			{ "VLWC", "Vapor-Liquid-FreeWaterCondenser" },
			{ "VLW", "Vapor-Liquid-FreeWater" },
			{ "VLLC", "Vapor-Liquid-LiquidCondenser" }

		public Composition()
		{
			//IL_0009: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0013: Expected O, but got Unknown
			InitializeComponent();
			SetVariables();
			SetControls();
			_bHasInputDialog = true;
			if (_imgBitmap == null)
			{
				Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly();
				Stream manifestResourceStream = Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly().GetManifestResourceStream("WpfPLBatchSep.Images.composition.ico");
				_imgBitmap = System.Drawing.Image.FromStream(manifestResourceStream);
			}
			CommandBinding commandBinding = new CommandBinding(OKCommand, ExecuteOKCommand, CanExecuteOKCommand);
			CommandBinding commandBinding2 = new CommandBinding(CancelCommand, ExecuteCancelCommand);
			base.CommandBindings.Add(commandBinding);
			base.CommandBindings.Add(commandBinding2);
			CurveList.Header = WpfPLBatchSepResources.Select_Comp;
		}

		List<string> IRibbonPlotWizardType.FieldDataOptionList(string sFieldName)
		{
			if (sFieldName.CompareTo(_stageOrderFieldId) == 0)
			{
				return new List<string> { _ascending, _descending };
			}
			if (sFieldName.CompareTo(_stageOnFieldId) == 0)
			{
				return new List<string> { _xAxis, _yAxis };
			}
			return null;
		}

		private void SetFaseRadBtns(string ValidPhases)
		{
			switch (ValidPhases)
			{
			case "Vapor-Liquid":
			case "Vapor-Liquid-LiquidCondenser":
			case "Vapor-Liquid-FreeWaterCondenser":
				_phaseRadBtnList[0].IsEnabled = true;
				_phaseRadBtnList[1].IsEnabled = true;
				break;
			case "Vapor-Liquid-Liquid":
				_phaseRadBtnList[0].IsEnabled = true;
				_phaseRadBtnList[1].IsEnabled = true;
				_phaseRadBtnList[2].IsEnabled = true;
				_phaseRadBtnList[3].IsEnabled = true;
				break;
			case "Vapor-Liquid-FreeWater":
				_phaseRadBtnList[0].IsEnabled = true;
				_phaseRadBtnList[1].IsEnabled = true;
				_phaseRadBtnList[2].IsEnabled = true;
				break;
			default:
				_phaseRadBtnList[0].IsEnabled = true;
				_phaseRadBtnList[1].IsEnabled = true;
				break;
			}
		}

		private int GetNStage()
		{
			int result = 0;
			object nodeCxs = _nodeCxs;
			CxsNode val = (CxsNode)((nodeCxs is CxsNode) ? nodeCxs : null);
			if (val != null)
			{
				CxsCase val2 = val.Case;
				string text = val.Path + ".Input.NSTAGE";
				if (val2.BatchGet(text, "value") != null)
				{
					result = (int)val2.BatchGet(text, "value");
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		private string GetBlockID()
		{
			string result = "";
			object nodeCxs = _nodeCxs;
			CxsNode val = (CxsNode)((nodeCxs is CxsNode) ? nodeCxs : null);
			if (val != null)
			{
				_ = val.Case;
				string path = val.Path;
				path = path.TrimEnd('.');
				try
				{
					foreach (string item in new List<string> { "Top.appModelV8.", "Blocks.", "Data.", "Controllers." })
					{
						path = path.Replace(item, "");
					}
					result = path;
				}
				catch
				{
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		private void InitDialogSettings()
		{
			object nodeCxs = _nodeCxs;
			CxsNode val = (CxsNode)((nodeCxs is CxsNode) ? nodeCxs : null);
			if (val == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			CxsCase val2 = val.Case;
			string text = val.Path + ".Input.VALIDPHASES";
			if (val2.BatchGet(text, "value") != null)
			{
				string key = (string)val2.BatchGet(text, "value");
				string faseRadBtns = "";
				if (sDicValidFases.ContainsKey(key))
				{
					faseRadBtns = sDicValidFases[key];
				}
				SetFaseRadBtns(faseRadBtns);
			}
		}

		private void InitCurveList()
		{
			object nodeCxs = _nodeCxs;
			CxsNode val = (CxsNode)((nodeCxs is CxsNode) ? nodeCxs : null);
			if (val == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			CxsCase val2 = val.Case;
			string path = val.Path;
			int length = path.IndexOf("Blocks", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
			string text = path.Substring(0, length);
			text += "Components.Specifications.Input.TYPE";
			object obj = val2.BatchGet(text, "child_ids");
			if (obj != null)
			{
				List<string> list = (from x in obj.ToString().Split()
					where !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(x)
					select x).ToList();
				if (list.Count > 0)
				{
					CurveList.IsCheckAll = false;
					CurveList.SetCurves(list.ToArray());
				}
			}
		}

		void IRibbonPlotWizardType.ShowInputDialog()
		{
			//IL_0017: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_001d: Expected O, but got Unknown
			InitCurveList();
			InitDialogSettings();
			_currentConfigValue = string.Empty;
			PlotWizardTemplate val = new PlotWizardTemplate();
			((FrameworkElement)(object)val).MinWidth = 400.0;
			((ContentControl)(object)val).Content = this;
			MMHelp.SetHelpFile((DependencyObject)(object)val, _help.GetHelpFileName());
			MMHelp.SetHelpId((DependencyObject)(object)val, (int?)(int)_help.GetHelpID());
			((FrameworkElement)(object)val).Cursor = Cursors.Arrow;
			radBtnLiquid.IsChecked = true;
			try
			{
				((Window)(object)val).ShowDialog();
			}
			catch (Exception e)
			{
				LogAndDisplayErrorMessage(e, bDisplayMessageOnException: true);
				((Window)(object)val).Close();
			}
			((ContentControl)(object)val).Content = null;
		}

		private void LogAndDisplayErrorMessage(Exception e, bool bDisplayMessageOnException)
		{
			//IL_007a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			string text = string.Format("Unhandled Exception{1}Exception Message: {0}{1}Excetion Source: {2}{1}StackTrace: {3}", e.Message, Environment.NewLine, e.Source, e.StackTrace);
			SystemEnvironment.Logger.Log(text, (Category)1, (Priority)1);
			ILoggerFacade logger = SystemEnvironment.Logger;
			DebugLogger val = (DebugLogger)(object)((logger is DebugLogger) ? logger : null);
			if (bDisplayMessageOnException && val != null && val.FilePath != null)
			{
				MMMessageBox.Show(string.Format(ApwnResources.PlotUnhandledException, val.FilePath), ApwnResources.AspenPlus, (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)16, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
			}
		}

		RibbonControlType IRibbonPlotWizardType.GetFieldCtrlType(string sField)
		{
			if (sField.CompareTo(_stageOrderFieldId) != 0 && sField.CompareTo(_stageOnFieldId) != 0)
			{
				return (RibbonControlType)0;
			}
			return (RibbonControlType)1;
		}

		void IRibbonPlotWizardType.GetFieldStyle(string sFieldName, ref string sCurrentVal)
		{
			if (sFieldName.CompareTo(_stageOrderFieldId) == 0)
			{
				if (_wizardPlot.PlotVal.ReverseXAxis)
				{
					sCurrentVal = _descending;
				}
				else
				{
					sCurrentVal = _ascending;
				}
			}
			else if (sFieldName.CompareTo(_stageOnFieldId) == 0)
			{
				if (_wizardPlot.PlotVal.SwapAxises)
				{
					sCurrentVal = _yAxis;
				}
				else
				{
					sCurrentVal = _xAxis;
				}
			}
		}

		void IRibbonPlotWizardType.SetFieldStyle(string sFieldName, string sValue)
		{
			if (sFieldName.CompareTo(_stageOrderFieldId) == 0)
			{
				if (sValue.CompareTo(_ascending) == 0)
				{
					_wizardPlot.PlotVal.ReverseXAxis = false;
				}
				else
				{
					_wizardPlot.PlotVal.ReverseXAxis = true;
				}
			}
			else if (sFieldName.CompareTo(_stageOnFieldId) == 0)
			{
				if (sValue.CompareTo(_yAxis) == 0)
				{
					_wizardPlot.PlotVal.SwapAxises = true;
				}
				else
				{
					_wizardPlot.PlotVal.SwapAxises = false;
				}
			}
		}

		void IRibbonPlotWizardType.GetConfigData(ref string sConfigValue)
		{
			sConfigValue = _currentConfigValue;
		}

		void IRibbonPlotWizardType.SetConfigData(string sValue)
		{
			_currentConfigValue = sValue;
		}

		void IRibbonPlotWizardType.Context(object HNode, ref object HWizardPlot, ref int ierror)
		{
			if (HWizardPlot == null || HNode == null)
			{
				ierror = 1;
				return;
			}
			_context = HNode as IHNode;
			_wizardPlot = HWizardPlot as IHWizardPlot;
			if (1 == 0)
			{
				ierror = 1;
				return;
			}
			string text = "";
			GetNStage();
			GetBlockID();
			switch (text)
			{
			case "Vapor-Liquid":
			case "Vapor-Liquid-LiquidCondenser":
			case "Vapor-Liquid-FreeWaterCondenser":
				_phaseRadBtnList[0].IsEnabled = true;
				_phaseRadBtnList[1].IsEnabled = true;
				break;
			case "Vapor-Liquid-Liquid":
				_phaseRadBtnList[0].IsEnabled = true;
				_phaseRadBtnList[1].IsEnabled = true;
				_phaseRadBtnList[2].IsEnabled = true;
				_phaseRadBtnList[3].IsEnabled = true;
				break;
			case "Vapor-Liquid-FreeWater":
				_phaseRadBtnList[0].IsEnabled = true;
				_phaseRadBtnList[1].IsEnabled = true;
				_phaseRadBtnList[2].IsEnabled = true;
				break;
			default:
				_phaseRadBtnList[0].IsEnabled = true;
				_phaseRadBtnList[1].IsEnabled = true;
				break;
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_currentConfigValue))
			{
				return;
			}
			_basisRadBtnList[0].IsChecked = true;
			for (int i = 0; i < _phaseRadBtnList.Count(); i++)
			{
				if (_phaseRadBtnList[i].IsEnabled)
				{
					_phaseRadBtnList[i].IsChecked = true;
					break;
				}
			}
		}

		public void UpdateWizardData()
		{
			int num = -1;
			string text = "";
			string text2 = "";
			for (int i = 0; i < _phaseRadBtnList.Count; i++)
			{
				if (_phaseRadBtnList[i].IsChecked == true)
				{
					num = i;
					text = _strTitle[i];
					text2 = _strYTitle[i];
					break;
				}
			}
			_sTitle = text;
			int num2 = -1;
			string text3 = string.Empty;
			string sTitleID = string.Empty;
			for (int j = 0; j < _basisRadBtnList.Count; j++)
			{
				if (_basisRadBtnList[j].IsChecked == true)
				{
					num2 = j;
					sTitleID = _strTBasis[j];
					text3 = _strYAxisTitle[j];
					break;
				}
			}
			_sTitleID = sTitleID;
			_wizardPlot.basis = num2 + 1;
			_wizardPlot.RemoveAllDependentVariable();
			string text4 = _varNames[num2, num];
			if (text4 != "")
			{
				List<string> list = new List<string>(CurveList.GetSelectedCurves());
				int count = list.Count;
				_VarName = text4;
				_selectedCurves.Clear();
				for (int k = 0; k < count; k++)
				{
					_selectedCurves.Add(list[k]);
				}
				int nStage = GetNStage();
				double[] array = new double[nStage];
				for (int l = 0; l < nStage; l++)
				{
					array[l] = 0.0;
				}
				if (_mvarControlContext != null)
				{
					_wizardPlot.PlotVal.NXVariables = 1;
					_wizardPlot.PlotVal.NVariables = count;
					_wizardPlot.PlotVal.NPoints = nStage;
					for (int m = 0; m < count; m++)
					{
						string text5 = list[m];
						((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_Label(m, text5);
						string text6 = text4 + "(\"" + text5 + "\")";
						array[0] = _mvarControlContext.Condenser.resolve(text6).value;
						for (int n = 2; n < nStage; n++)
						{
							array[n - 1] = _mvarControlContext.Stage(n).Resolve(text6).value;
						}
						array[nStage - 1] = _mvarControlContext.Pot.resolve(text6).value;
						for (int num3 = 0; num3 < nStage; num3++)
						{
							double num4 = num3 + 1;
							((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_XData(0, num3, (short)0, (object)num4);
							((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_YData(m, num3, (short)0, (object)array[num3]);
						}
					}
				}
			}
			_wizardPlot.PlotVal.Title = text;
			((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_YAxisTitle((object)null, text2 + " " + text3);
			_currentConfigValue = SaveConfigValue();
		}

		private string GetTimeConfig()
		{
			string empty = string.Empty;
			string text = string.Empty;
			string text2 = string.Empty;
			_sProfileConfig = string.Empty;
			_sYAxisName = string.Empty;
			int count = _selectedCurves.Count;
			int nStage = GetNStage();
			string blockID = GetBlockID();
			if (count > 0 && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_VarName) && nStage > 0 && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(blockID))
			{
				_sProfileConfig = sProfilePlot;
				for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
				{
					string text3 = _selectedCurves[i];
					string text4 = _VarName + "(*," + text3 + ")";
					text = text + "$" + text4;
					text4 = text4 + "$" + nStage;
					_sProfileConfig = _sProfileConfig + "!" + text4;
					_sYAxisName = _sYAxisName + "!" + text3;
					text2 = text2 + "$" + sDicVarNameToPrefix[_VarName] + "(" + text3 + ")";
				}
				_sYAxisName = _sYAxisName.Remove(0, 1);
				_sTitle = _sTitle.Replace("%ID%", blockID);
				_sTitle = _sTitle.Replace("%TID%", _sTitleID);
				string sTitle = _sTitle;
				int num = sTitle.IndexOf(':');
				sTitle = sTitle.Substring(num + 1);
				empty = empty + "Type:$" + sProfilePlot;
				empty = empty + "!VarPaths:" + text;
				empty = empty + "!nStage:$" + nStage;
				empty = empty + "!PlotTitle:$" + sTitle;
				empty = empty + "!YAxisNames:" + text2;
				empty = empty + "!BlockPath:$" + blockID;
				empty = empty + "!TabTitle:$" + _sTitle;
				empty += "!PlotSettings:$<Scpc ShowMultipleYAxes=\"false\">\r\n\t                    <OtherSettings>\r\n\t\t                    <ShowTitle>true</ShowTitle>\r\n\t\t                    <ShowLegend>true</ShowLegend>\r\n\t                    </OtherSettings>\r\n                    </Scpc>";
				_sProfileConfig = empty;
			}
			return _sProfileConfig;
		}

		private void ExecuteOKCommand(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			UpdateWizardData();
			_ = _currentConfigValue;
			_currentConfigValue = GetTimeConfig();
			_help.CloseForm((UserControl)this, (bool?)true);
		}

		private void CanExecuteOKCommand(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			int num = CurveList.GetSelectedCurves().Count();
			if (num == CurveList.Curves.Count)
			{
				CurveList.IsCheckAll = true;
			}
			else
			{
				string[] selectedCurves = CurveList.GetSelectedCurves();
				CurveList.IsCheckAll = false;
				CurveList.SetSelectedCurves(selectedCurves);
			}
			e.CanExecute = num != 0;
		}

		private void ExecuteCancelCommand(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			_currentConfigValue = string.Empty;
			_sYAxisName = string.Empty;
			_sTitle = string.Empty;
			_help.CloseForm((UserControl)this, (bool?)false);
		}

		private void SetVariables()
		{
			_varNames = new string[2, 4]
			{
				{ "Y", "X", "X1", "X2" },
				{ "YM", "XM", "XM1", "XM2" }
			};
			_strTitle = new string[4]
			{
				WpfPLBatchSepResources.Composition_Title_VaporA + " " + WpfPLBatchSepResources.Profile,
				WpfPLBatchSepResources.Composition_Title_LiquidA + " " + WpfPLBatchSepResources.Profile,
				WpfPLBatchSepResources.Composition_Title_1stLiquidA + " " + WpfPLBatchSepResources.Profile,
				WpfPLBatchSepResources.Composition_Title_2ndLiquidA + " " + WpfPLBatchSepResources.Profile
			};
			_strTBasis = new string[2]
			{
				WpfPLBatchSepResources.Mole,
				WpfPLBatchSepResources.Mass
			};
			_strYTitle = new string[4] { "Y", "X", "X1", "X2" };
			_strYAxisTitle = new string[2]
			{
				WpfPLBatchSepResources.Composition_Title_Mole,
				WpfPLBatchSepResources.Composition_Title_Mass
			};
		}

		private void SetControls()
		{
			_phaseRadBtnList = new List<RadioButton>();
			_phaseRadBtnList.Add(radBtnVapor);
			_phaseRadBtnList.Add(radBtnLiquid);
			_phaseRadBtnList.Add(radBtn1stLiquid);
			_phaseRadBtnList.Add(radBtn2ndLiquid);
			for (int i = 0; i < _phaseRadBtnList.Count; i++)
			{
				_phaseRadBtnList[i].IsEnabled = false;
				_phaseRadBtnList[i].IsChecked = false;
			}
			_basisRadBtnList = new List<RadioButton>();
			_basisRadBtnList.Add(radBtnMole);
			_basisRadBtnList.Add(radBtnMass);
		}

		private string SaveConfigValue()
		{
			_ = string.Empty;
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			stringBuilder.Append("PhaseOption=");
			for (int i = 0; i < _phaseRadBtnList.Count; i++)
			{
				if (_phaseRadBtnList[i].IsChecked == true)
				{
					stringBuilder.Append(i.ToString());
					break;
				}
			}
			stringBuilder.Append(";");
			stringBuilder.Append("PhaseEnable=");
			int num = 0;
			for (int j = 0; j < _phaseRadBtnList.Count; j++)
			{
				if (_phaseRadBtnList[j].IsEnabled)
				{
					if (num > 0)
					{
						stringBuilder.Append(",");
					}
					stringBuilder.Append(j.ToString());
					num++;
				}
			}
			stringBuilder.Append(";");
			stringBuilder.Append("BasisOption=");
			for (int k = 0; k < _basisRadBtnList.Count; k++)
			{
				if (_basisRadBtnList[k].IsChecked == true)
				{
					stringBuilder.Append(k.ToString());
					break;
				}
			}
			stringBuilder.Append(";");
			stringBuilder.Append("BasisEnable=");
			num = 0;
			for (int l = 0; l < _basisRadBtnList.Count; l++)
			{
				if (_basisRadBtnList[l].IsEnabled)
				{
					if (num > 0)
					{
						stringBuilder.Append(",");
					}
					stringBuilder.Append(l.ToString());
					num++;
				}
			}
			stringBuilder.Append(";");
			stringBuilder.Append("CompSelection=");
			List<string> list = new List<string>(CurveList.GetSelectedCurves());
			int count = list.Count;
			for (int m = 0; m < count; m++)
			{
				if (m > 0)
				{
					stringBuilder.Append(",");
				}
				string value = list[m];
				stringBuilder.Append(value);
			}
			stringBuilder.Append(";");
			stringBuilder.Append("CheckAll=");
			stringBuilder.Append(CurveList.IsCheckAll);
			stringBuilder.Append(";");
			return stringBuilder.ToString();
		}

		private void RestoreConfigValue(string ConfigString)
		{
			IHNode iHNode = null;
			string[] array = ConfigString.Split(';');
			int num = 0;
			string value = array[num].Substring(array[num].IndexOf('=') + 1);
			int num2 = Convert.ToInt16(value);
			_phaseRadBtnList[num2].IsChecked = true;
			iHNode = _context.FindNode(_varNames[0, num2]);
			num++;
			value = array[num].Substring(array[num].IndexOf('=') + 1);
			string[] array2 = value.Split(',');
			foreach (string value2 in array2)
			{
				_phaseRadBtnList[Convert.ToInt16(value2)].IsEnabled = true;
			}
			num++;
			value = array[num].Substring(array[num].IndexOf('=') + 1);
			_basisRadBtnList[Convert.ToInt16(value)].IsChecked = true;
			num++;
			value = array[num].Substring(array[num].IndexOf('=') + 1);
			array2 = value.Split(',');
			foreach (string value3 in array2)
			{
				_basisRadBtnList[Convert.ToInt16(value3)].IsEnabled = true;
			}
			num++;
			value = array[num].Substring(array[num].IndexOf('=') + 1);
			string[] array3 = value.Split(',');
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			if (iHNode != null && iHNode.Elements.Count > 0)
			{
				IHNodeCol elements = iHNode.Elements[0, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements;
				if (elements != null && elements.Count > 0)
				{
					for (int j = 0; j < elements.Count; j++)
					{
						string item = elements.get_Label(0, j, (object)null);
						list.Add(item);
					}
					CurveList.IsCheckAll = false;
					CurveList.SetCurves(list.ToArray());
				}
			}
			List<string> list2 = new List<string>();
			array2 = array3;
			foreach (string item2 in array2)
			{
				list2.Add(item2);
			}
			CurveList.SetSelectedCurves(list2.ToArray());
			num++;
			value = array[num].Substring(array[num].IndexOf('=') + 1);
			CurveList.IsCheckAll = Convert.ToBoolean(value);
		}

		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "4.0.0.0")]
		public void InitializeComponent()
		{
			if (!_contentLoaded)
			{
				_contentLoaded = true;
				Uri resourceLocator = new Uri("/WpfPLBatchSep;component/composition.xaml", UriKind.Relative);
				Application.LoadComponent(this, resourceLocator);
			}
		}

		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "4.0.0.0")]
		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		void IComponentConnector.Connect(int connectionId, object target)
		{
			switch (connectionId)
			{
			case 1:
				radBtnVapor = (RadioButton)target;
				break;
			case 2:
				radBtnLiquid = (RadioButton)target;
				break;
			case 3:
				radBtn1stLiquid = (RadioButton)target;
				break;
			case 4:
				radBtn2ndLiquid = (RadioButton)target;
				break;
			case 5:
				radBtnMole = (RadioButton)target;
				break;
			case 6:
				radBtnMass = (RadioButton)target;
				break;
			case 7:
				CurveList = (CurvesSelectorControl)target;
				break;
			default:
				_contentLoaded = true;
				break;
			}
		}

	[ProgId("WpfPLBatchSep.RibbonPlotWizardMgr")]
	public class RibbonPlotWizardMgr : IRibbonPlotWizardMgr
	{
		private static string[] supportedTypes = new string[12]
		{
			"Pot Temperature", "Pot Pressure", "Pot Composition", "Receiver Composition", "Pot Holdup", "Receiver Holdup", "Pot Duty", "Pot Reaction Rates", "Temperature Profile", "Pressure Profile",
			"Composition Profile", "Custommm"
		};

		private IHNode _hnode;

		private IHWizardPlot _wizardPlot;

		private static List<PWRPlotType> _lspwrTypes = new List<PWRPlotType>();

		private PWRParser _pwrParser = new PWRParser();

		private Assembly _asm;

		private Dictionary<string, IRibbonPlotWizardType> _dicTagIdToPlotType;

		private IHWizardPlot _tempWizard = (CHWizardPlot)Activator.CreateInstance(Marshal.GetTypeFromCLSID(new Guid("9E14C11F-6706-4067-8112-35CE9468F3A5")));

		public RibbonPlotWizardMgr()
		{
			//IL_0001: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_000b: Expected O, but got Unknown
			Initialize();
		}

		long IRibbonPlotWizardMgr.SupportedPlotTypes(ref List<string> lsTagIds)
		{
			lsTagIds.Clear();
			for (int i = 0; i < _lspwrTypes.Count; i++)
			{
				PWRPlotType val = _lspwrTypes[i];
				IRibbonPlotWizardType iplotType = null;
				_tempWizard.Clear();
				_pwrParser.FillDefaultPlotVal(val, ref _hnode, ref _tempWizard);
				if (DoesPlotTypeHaveData(val.TagId, ref iplotType, ref _tempWizard))
				{
					lsTagIds.Add(val.TagId);
				}
			}
			return _lspwrTypes.Count;
		}

		long IRibbonPlotWizardMgr.GetPlotType(string sTagId, ref IRibbonPlotWizardType plwType)
		{
			if (_dicTagIdToPlotType.ContainsKey(sTagId))
			{
				plwType = _dicTagIdToPlotType[sTagId];
				if (plwType == null)
				{
					return 0L;
				}
				PWRPlotType val = null;
				_pwrParser.FindPlotType(sTagId, ref _lspwrTypes, ref val);
				_pwrParser.FillDefaultPlotVal(val, ref _hnode, ref _wizardPlot);
			}
			return 0L;
		}

		void IRibbonPlotWizardMgr.SetContext(object oNode, ref object oWizard)
		{
			_hnode = oNode as IHNode;
			_wizardPlot = oWizard as IHWizardPlot;
		}

		void IRibbonPlotWizardMgr.ShowInputDialog(string sPlotName)
		{
		}

		private void InitializeWizardPlot()
		{
		}

		private void Initialize()
		{
			_asm = Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly();
			StreamReader streamReader = new StreamReader(Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly().GetManifestResourceStream("WpfPLBatchSep.Text.BatchSeppwr.txt"));
			_lspwrTypes.Clear();
			_pwrParser.ParsePwrFile(streamReader, ref _lspwrTypes);
			_dicTagIdToPlotType = new Dictionary<string, IRibbonPlotWizardType>();
			for (int i = 0; i < _lspwrTypes.Count; i++)
			{
				string tagId = _lspwrTypes[i].TagId;
				if (tagId == supportedTypes[0])
				{
					IRibbonPlotWizardType value = (IRibbonPlotWizardType)(object)new PotTemperature();
					_dicTagIdToPlotType.Add(tagId, value);
				}
				else if (tagId == supportedTypes[1])
				{
					IRibbonPlotWizardType value = (IRibbonPlotWizardType)(object)new PotPressure();
					_dicTagIdToPlotType.Add(tagId, value);
				}
				else if (tagId == supportedTypes[2])
				{
					IRibbonPlotWizardType value = (IRibbonPlotWizardType)(object)new PotComposition();
					_dicTagIdToPlotType.Add(tagId, value);
				}
				else if (tagId == supportedTypes[3])
				{
					IRibbonPlotWizardType value = (IRibbonPlotWizardType)(object)new ReceiverComposition();
					_dicTagIdToPlotType.Add(tagId, value);
				}
				else if (tagId == supportedTypes[4])
				{
					IRibbonPlotWizardType value = (IRibbonPlotWizardType)(object)new PotHoldup();
					_dicTagIdToPlotType.Add(tagId, value);
				}
				else if (tagId == supportedTypes[5])
				{
					IRibbonPlotWizardType value = (IRibbonPlotWizardType)(object)new ReceiverHoldup();
					_dicTagIdToPlotType.Add(tagId, value);
				}
				else if (tagId == supportedTypes[6])
				{
					IRibbonPlotWizardType value = (IRibbonPlotWizardType)(object)new PotDuty();
					_dicTagIdToPlotType.Add(tagId, value);
				}
				else if (tagId == supportedTypes[7])
				{
					IRibbonPlotWizardType value = (IRibbonPlotWizardType)(object)new PotReactionRates();
					_dicTagIdToPlotType.Add(tagId, value);
				}
				else if (tagId == supportedTypes[8])
				{
					IRibbonPlotWizardType value = (IRibbonPlotWizardType)(object)new Temperature();
					_dicTagIdToPlotType.Add(tagId, value);
				}
				else if (tagId == supportedTypes[9])
				{
					IRibbonPlotWizardType value = (IRibbonPlotWizardType)(object)new Pressure();
					_dicTagIdToPlotType.Add(tagId, value);
				}
				else if (tagId == supportedTypes[10])
				{
					IRibbonPlotWizardType value = (IRibbonPlotWizardType)(object)new Composition();
					_dicTagIdToPlotType.Add(tagId, value);
				}
				else if (tagId == supportedTypes[11])
				{
					IRibbonPlotWizardType value = (IRibbonPlotWizardType)(object)new Custom();
					_dicTagIdToPlotType.Add(tagId, value);
				}
			}
		}

		private bool DoesPlotTypeHaveData(string sTagId, ref IRibbonPlotWizardType iplotType, ref IHWizardPlot iWizardPlot)
		{
			int num = 0;
			if (_dicTagIdToPlotType.ContainsKey(sTagId))
			{
				iplotType = _dicTagIdToPlotType[sTagId];
				if (iplotType == null)
				{
					return false;
				}
				object obj = iWizardPlot;
				iplotType.Context((object)_hnode, ref obj, ref num);
				if (num == 0)
				{
					return true;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}
	}

		List<string> IRibbonPlotWizardType.FieldDataOptionList(string sFieldName)
		{

			{

		private void SetFaseRadBtns(string ValidPhases)
		{
			switch (ValidPhases)
			{
			case "Vapor-Liquid":
			case "Vapor-Liquid-LiquidCondenser":
			case "Vapor-Liquid-FreeWaterCondenser":
				_phaseRadBtnList[0].IsEnabled = true;
				_phaseRadBtnList[1].IsEnabled = true;
				break;
			case "Vapor-Liquid-Liquid":
				_phaseRadBtnList[0].IsEnabled = true;
				_phaseRadBtnList[1].IsEnabled = true;
				_phaseRadBtnList[2].IsEnabled = true;
				_phaseRadBtnList[3].IsEnabled = true;
				break;
			case "Vapor-Liquid-FreeWater":
				_phaseRadBtnList[0].IsEnabled = true;
				_phaseRadBtnList[1].IsEnabled = true;
				_phaseRadBtnList[2].IsEnabled = true;
				break;
			default:
				_phaseRadBtnList[0].IsEnabled = true;
				_phaseRadBtnList[1].IsEnabled = true;
				break;
			}
		}

		private int GetNStage()
		{
			int result = 0;
			object nodeCxs = _nodeCxs;
			CxsNode val = (CxsNode)((nodeCxs is CxsNode) ? nodeCxs : null);
			if (val != null)
			{
				CxsCase val2 = val.Case;
				string text = val.Path + ".Input.NSTAGE";
				if (val2.BatchGet(text, "value") != null)
				{
					result = (int)val2.BatchGet(text, "value");
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		private string GetBlockID()
		{
			string result = "";
			object nodeCxs = _nodeCxs;
			CxsNode val = (CxsNode)((nodeCxs is CxsNode) ? nodeCxs : null);
			if (val != null)
			{
				_ = val.Case;
				string path = val.Path;
				path = path.TrimEnd('.');
				try
				{
					foreach (string item in new List<string> { "Top.appModelV8.", "Blocks.", "Data.", "Controllers." })
					{
						path = path.Replace(item, "");
					}
					result = path;
				}
				catch
				{
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		private void InitDialogSettings()
		{
			object nodeCxs = _nodeCxs;
			CxsNode val = (CxsNode)((nodeCxs is CxsNode) ? nodeCxs : null);
			if (val == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			CxsCase val2 = val.Case;
			string text = val.Path + ".Input.VALIDPHASES";
			if (val2.BatchGet(text, "value") != null)
			{
				string key = (string)val2.BatchGet(text, "value");
				string faseRadBtns = "";
				if (sDicValidFases.ContainsKey(key))
				{
					faseRadBtns = sDicValidFases[key];
				}
				SetFaseRadBtns(faseRadBtns);
			}
		}

		void IRibbonPlotWizardType.ShowInputDialog()
		{
			//IL_0011: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0017: Expected O, but got Unknown
			InitDialogSettings();
			_currentConfigValue = string.Empty;
			PlotWizardTemplate val = new PlotWizardTemplate();
			((FrameworkElement)(object)val).MinWidth = 400.0;
			((ContentControl)(object)val).Content = this;
			MMHelp.SetHelpFile((DependencyObject)(object)val, _help.GetHelpFileName());
			MMHelp.SetHelpId((DependencyObject)(object)val, (int?)(int)_help.GetHelpID());
			((FrameworkElement)(object)val).Cursor = Cursors.Arrow;
			radBtnLiquid.IsChecked = true;
			try
			{
				((Window)(object)val).ShowDialog();
			}
			catch (Exception e)
			{
				LogAndDisplayErrorMessage(e, bDisplayMessageOnException: true);
				((Window)(object)val).Close();
			}
			((ContentControl)(object)val).Content = null;
		}

		private void LogAndDisplayErrorMessage(Exception e, bool bDisplayMessageOnException)
		{
			//IL_007a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			string text = string.Format("Unhandled Exception{1}Exception Message: {0}{1}Excetion Source: {2}{1}StackTrace: {3}", e.Message, Environment.NewLine, e.Source, e.StackTrace);
			SystemEnvironment.Logger.Log(text, (Category)1, (Priority)1);
			ILoggerFacade logger = SystemEnvironment.Logger;
			DebugLogger val = (DebugLogger)(object)((logger is DebugLogger) ? logger : null);
			if (bDisplayMessageOnException && val != null && val.FilePath != null)
			{
				MMMessageBox.Show(string.Format(ApwnResources.PlotUnhandledException, val.FilePath), ApwnResources.AspenPlus, (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)16, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
			}
		}

		RibbonControlType IRibbonPlotWizardType.GetFieldCtrlType(string sField)
		{
			if (sField.CompareTo(_stageOrderFieldId) != 0 && sField.CompareTo(_stageOnFieldId) != 0)
			{
				return (RibbonControlType)0;
			}
			return (RibbonControlType)1;
		}

		void IRibbonPlotWizardType.GetFieldStyle(string sFieldName, ref string sCurrentVal)
		{
			if (sFieldName.CompareTo(_stageOrderFieldId) == 0)
			{
				if (_wizardPlot.PlotVal.ReverseXAxis)
				{
					sCurrentVal = _descending;
				}
				else
				{
					sCurrentVal = _ascending;
				}
			}
			else if (sFieldName.CompareTo(_stageOnFieldId) == 0)
			{
				if (_wizardPlot.PlotVal.SwapAxises)
				{
					sCurrentVal = _yAxis;
				}
				else
				{
					sCurrentVal = _xAxis;
				}
			}
		}

		void IRibbonPlotWizardType.SetFieldStyle(string sFieldName, string sValue)
		{
			if (sFieldName.CompareTo(_stageOrderFieldId) == 0)
			{
				if (sValue.CompareTo(_ascending) == 0)
				{
					_wizardPlot.PlotVal.ReverseXAxis = false;
				}
				else
				{
					_wizardPlot.PlotVal.ReverseXAxis = true;
				}
			}
			else if (sFieldName.CompareTo(_stageOnFieldId) == 0)
			{
				if (sValue.CompareTo(_yAxis) == 0)
				{
					_wizardPlot.PlotVal.SwapAxises = true;
				}
				else
				{
					_wizardPlot.PlotVal.SwapAxises = false;
				}
			}
		}

		void IRibbonPlotWizardType.GetConfigData(ref string sConfigValue)
		{
			sConfigValue = _currentConfigValue;
		}

		void IRibbonPlotWizardType.SetConfigData(string sValue)
		{
			_currentConfigValue = sValue;
		}

		void IRibbonPlotWizardType.Context(object HNode, ref object HWizardPlot, ref int ierror)
		{
			if (HWizardPlot == null || HNode == null)
			{
				ierror = 1;
				return;
			}
			_context = HNode as IHNode;
			_wizardPlot = HWizardPlot as IHWizardPlot;
			_bHasInputDialog = false;
			int nStage = GetNStage();
			string blockID = GetBlockID();
			_sYAxisName = WpfPLBatchSepResources.PotTemperature;
			_sTitle = WpfPLBatchSepResources.BlockTitle + WpfPLBatchSepResources.PotTemperature;
			_sTitle = _sTitle.Replace("%ID%", blockID);
			string text = "";
			text = text + "Type:$" + sProfilePlot;
			text += "!VarPaths:$T(*)";
			text = text + "!nStage:$" + nStage;
			text = text + "!PlotTitle:$" + WpfPLBatchSepResources.PotTemperature;
			text = text + "!YAxisNames:$" + _sYAxisName;
			text = text + "!BlockPath:$" + blockID;
			text = text + "!TabTitle:$" + _sTitle;
			text += "!PlotSettings:$<Scpc ShowMultipleYAxes=\"true\">\r\n\t                    <OtherSettings>\r\n\t\t                    <ShowTitle>true</ShowTitle>\r\n\t\t                    <ShowLegend>false</ShowLegend>\r\n\t                    </OtherSettings>\r\n                    </Scpc>";
			_currentConfigValue = text;
			if (1 == 0)
			{
				ierror = 1;
			}
		}

		public void UpdateWizardData()
		{
			int num = -1;
			string text = "";
			string text2 = "";
			for (int i = 0; i < _phaseRadBtnList.Count; i++)
			{
				if (_phaseRadBtnList[i].IsChecked == true)
				{
					num = i;
					text = _strTitle[i];
					text2 = _strYTitle[i];
					break;
				}
			}
			_sTitle = text;
			int num2 = -1;
			string text3 = string.Empty;
			string sTitleID = string.Empty;
			for (int j = 0; j < _basisRadBtnList.Count; j++)
			{
				if (_basisRadBtnList[j].IsChecked == true)
				{
					num2 = j;
					sTitleID = _strTBasis[j];
					text3 = _strYAxisTitle[j];
					break;
				}
			}
			_sTitleID = sTitleID;
			_wizardPlot.basis = num2 + 1;
			_wizardPlot.RemoveAllDependentVariable();
			string text4 = _varNames[num2, num];
			if (text4 != "")
			{
				List<string> list = new List<string>(CurveList.GetSelectedCurves());
				int count = list.Count;
				_VarName = text4;
				_selectedCurves.Clear();
				for (int k = 0; k < count; k++)
				{
					_selectedCurves.Add(list[k]);
				}
				int nStage = GetNStage();
				double[] array = new double[nStage];
				_wizardPlot.PlotVal.NXVariables = 1;
				_wizardPlot.PlotVal.NVariables = count;
				_wizardPlot.PlotVal.NPoints = nStage;
				for (int l = 0; l < count; l++)
				{
					string text5 = list[l];
					((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_Label(l, text5);
					string text6 = text4 + "(\"" + text5 + "\")";
					array[0] = _mvarControlContext.Condenser.resolve(text6).value;
					for (int m = 2; m < nStage; m++)
					{
						array[m - 1] = _mvarControlContext.Stage(m).Resolve(text6).value;
					}
					array[nStage - 1] = _mvarControlContext.Pot.resolve(text6).value;
					for (int n = 0; n < nStage; n++)
					{
						double num3 = n + 1;
						((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_XData(0, n, (short)0, (object)num3);
						((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_YData(l, n, (short)0, (object)array[n]);
					}
				}
			}
			_wizardPlot.PlotVal.Title = text;
			((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_YAxisTitle((object)null, text2 + " " + text3);
			_currentConfigValue = SaveConfigValue();
		}

		private string GetTimeConfig()
		{
			string empty = string.Empty;
			string text = string.Empty;
			string text2 = string.Empty;
			_sProfileConfig = string.Empty;
			_sYAxisName = string.Empty;
			int count = _selectedCurves.Count;
			int nStage = GetNStage();
			string blockID = GetBlockID();
			if (count > 0 && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_VarName) && nStage > 0 && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(blockID))
			{
				_sProfileConfig = sProfilePlot;
				for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
				{
					string text3 = _selectedCurves[i];
					string text4 = _VarName + "(*," + text3 + ")";
					text = text + "$" + text4;
					text4 = text4 + "$" + nStage;
					_sProfileConfig = _sProfileConfig + "!" + text4;
					_sYAxisName = _sYAxisName + "!" + text3;
					text2 = text2 + "$" + sDicVarNameToPrefix[_VarName] + "(" + text3 + ")";
				}
				_sYAxisName = _sYAxisName.Remove(0, 1);
				_sTitle = _sTitle.Replace("%ID%", blockID);
				_sTitle = _sTitle.Replace("%TID%", _sTitleID);
				string sTitle = _sTitle;
				int num = sTitle.IndexOf(':');
				sTitle = sTitle.Substring(num + 1);
				empty = empty + "Type:$" + sProfilePlot;
				empty = empty + "!VarPaths:" + text;
				empty = empty + "!nStage:$" + nStage;
				empty = empty + "!PlotTitle:$" + sTitle;
				empty = empty + "!YAxisNames:" + text2;
				empty = empty + "!BlockPath:$" + blockID;
				empty = empty + "!TabTitle:$" + _sTitle;
				empty += "!PlotSettings:$<Scpc ShowMultipleYAxes=\"false\">\r\n\t                    <OtherSettings>\r\n\t\t                    <ShowTitle>true</ShowTitle>\r\n\t\t                    <ShowLegend>true</ShowLegend>\r\n\t                    </OtherSettings>\r\n                    </Scpc>";
				_sProfileConfig = empty;
			}
			return _sProfileConfig;
		}

		private void ExecuteOKCommand(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			UpdateWizardData();
			_ = _currentConfigValue;
			_currentConfigValue = GetTimeConfig();
			_help.CloseForm((UserControl)this, (bool?)true);
		}

		private void CanExecuteOKCommand(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			int num = CurveList.GetSelectedCurves().Count();
			if (num == CurveList.Curves.Count)
			{
				CurveList.IsCheckAll = true;
			}
			else
			{
				string[] selectedCurves = CurveList.GetSelectedCurves();
				CurveList.IsCheckAll = false;
				CurveList.SetSelectedCurves(selectedCurves);
			}
			e.CanExecute = num != 0;
		}

		private void ExecuteCancelCommand(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			_currentConfigValue = string.Empty;
			_sYAxisName = string.Empty;
			_sTitle = string.Empty;
			_help.CloseForm((UserControl)this, (bool?)false);
		}

		private void SetVariables()
		{
			_varNames = new string[2, 4]
			{
				{ "Y", "X", "X1", "X2" },
				{ "YM", "XM", "XM1", "XM2" }
			};
			_strTitle = new string[4]
			{
				WpfPLBatchSepResources.Composition_Title_VaporA + " " + WpfPLBatchSepResources.Profile,
				WpfPLBatchSepResources.Composition_Title_LiquidA + " " + WpfPLBatchSepResources.Profile,
				WpfPLBatchSepResources.Composition_Title_1stLiquidA + " " + WpfPLBatchSepResources.Profile,
				WpfPLBatchSepResources.Composition_Title_2ndLiquidA + " " + WpfPLBatchSepResources.Profile
			};
			_strTBasis = new string[2]
			{
				WpfPLBatchSepResources.Mole,
				WpfPLBatchSepResources.Mass
			};
			_strYTitle = new string[4] { "Y", "X", "X1", "X2" };
			_strYAxisTitle = new string[2]
			{
				WpfPLBatchSepResources.Composition_Title_Mole,
				WpfPLBatchSepResources.Composition_Title_Mass
			};
		}

		private void SetControls()
		{
			_phaseRadBtnList = new List<RadioButton>();
			_phaseRadBtnList.Add(radBtnVapor);
			_phaseRadBtnList.Add(radBtnLiquid);
			_phaseRadBtnList.Add(radBtn1stLiquid);
			_phaseRadBtnList.Add(radBtn2ndLiquid);
			for (int i = 0; i < _phaseRadBtnList.Count; i++)
			{
				_phaseRadBtnList[i].IsEnabled = false;
				_phaseRadBtnList[i].IsChecked = false;
			}
			_basisRadBtnList = new List<RadioButton>();
			_basisRadBtnList.Add(radBtnMole);
			_basisRadBtnList.Add(radBtnMass);
		}

		private string SaveConfigValue()
		{
			_ = string.Empty;
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			stringBuilder.Append("PhaseOption=");
			for (int i = 0; i < _phaseRadBtnList.Count; i++)
			{
				if (_phaseRadBtnList[i].IsChecked == true)
				{
					stringBuilder.Append(i.ToString());
					break;
				}
			}
			stringBuilder.Append(";");
			stringBuilder.Append("PhaseEnable=");
			int num = 0;
			for (int j = 0; j < _phaseRadBtnList.Count; j++)
			{
				if (_phaseRadBtnList[j].IsEnabled)
				{
					if (num > 0)
					{
						stringBuilder.Append(",");
					}
					stringBuilder.Append(j.ToString());
					num++;
				}
			}
			stringBuilder.Append(";");
			stringBuilder.Append("BasisOption=");
			for (int k = 0; k < _basisRadBtnList.Count; k++)
			{
				if (_basisRadBtnList[k].IsChecked == true)
				{
					stringBuilder.Append(k.ToString());
					break;
				}
			}
			stringBuilder.Append(";");
			stringBuilder.Append("BasisEnable=");
			num = 0;
			for (int l = 0; l < _basisRadBtnList.Count; l++)
			{
				if (_basisRadBtnList[l].IsEnabled)
				{
					if (num > 0)
					{
						stringBuilder.Append(",");
					}
					stringBuilder.Append(l.ToString());
					num++;
				}
			}
			stringBuilder.Append(";");
			stringBuilder.Append("CompSelection=");
			List<string> list = new List<string>(CurveList.GetSelectedCurves());
			int count = list.Count;
			for (int m = 0; m < count; m++)
			{
				if (m > 0)
				{
					stringBuilder.Append(",");
				}
				string value = list[m];
				stringBuilder.Append(value);
			}
			stringBuilder.Append(";");
			stringBuilder.Append("CheckAll=");
			stringBuilder.Append(CurveList.IsCheckAll);
			stringBuilder.Append(";");
			return stringBuilder.ToString();
		}

		private void RestoreConfigValue(string ConfigString)
		{
			IHNode iHNode = null;
			string[] array = ConfigString.Split(';');
			int num = 0;
			string value = array[num].Substring(array[num].IndexOf('=') + 1);
			int num2 = Convert.ToInt16(value);
			_phaseRadBtnList[num2].IsChecked = true;
			iHNode = _context.FindNode(_varNames[0, num2]);
			num++;
			value = array[num].Substring(array[num].IndexOf('=') + 1);
			string[] array2 = value.Split(',');
			foreach (string value2 in array2)
			{
				_phaseRadBtnList[Convert.ToInt16(value2)].IsEnabled = true;
			}
			num++;
			value = array[num].Substring(array[num].IndexOf('=') + 1);
			_basisRadBtnList[Convert.ToInt16(value)].IsChecked = true;
			num++;
			value = array[num].Substring(array[num].IndexOf('=') + 1);
			array2 = value.Split(',');
			foreach (string value3 in array2)
			{
				_basisRadBtnList[Convert.ToInt16(value3)].IsEnabled = true;
			}
			num++;
			value = array[num].Substring(array[num].IndexOf('=') + 1);
			string[] array3 = value.Split(',');
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			if (iHNode != null && iHNode.Elements.Count > 0)
			{
				IHNodeCol elements = iHNode.Elements[0, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements;
				if (elements != null && elements.Count > 0)
				{
					for (int j = 0; j < elements.Count; j++)
					{
						string item = elements.get_Label(0, j, (object)null);
						list.Add(item);
					}
					CurveList.IsCheckAll = false;
					CurveList.SetCurves(list.ToArray());
				}
			}
			List<string> list2 = new List<string>();
			array2 = array3;
			foreach (string item2 in array2)
			{
				list2.Add(item2);
			}
			CurveList.SetSelectedCurves(list2.ToArray());
			num++;
			value = array[num].Substring(array[num].IndexOf('=') + 1);
			CurveList.IsCheckAll = Convert.ToBoolean(value);
		}

		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "4.0.0.0")]
		public void InitializeComponent()
		{
			if (!_contentLoaded)
			{
				_contentLoaded = true;
				Uri resourceLocator = new Uri("/WpfPLBatchSep;component/customplot.xaml", UriKind.Relative);
				Application.LoadComponent(this, resourceLocator);
			}
		}

		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "4.0.0.0")]
		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		void IComponentConnector.Connect(int connectionId, object target)
		{
			switch (connectionId)
			{
			case 1:
				radBtnVapor = (RadioButton)target;
				break;
			case 2:
				radBtnLiquid = (RadioButton)target;
				break;
			case 3:
				radBtn1stLiquid = (RadioButton)target;
				break;
			case 4:
				radBtn2ndLiquid = (RadioButton)target;
				break;
			case 5:
				radBtnMole = (RadioButton)target;
				break;
			case 6:
				radBtnMass = (RadioButton)target;
				break;
			case 7:
				CurveList = (CurvesSelectorControl)target;
				break;
			default:
				_contentLoaded = true;
				break;
			}
		}
	}

		public PotComposition()
		{
			//IL_0009: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0013: Expected O, but got Unknown
			InitializeComponent();
			SetVariables();
			SetControls();
			_bHasInputDialog = true;

			base.CommandBindings.Add(commandBinding2);

		}

		private void SetFaseRadBtns(string ValidPhases)
		{
			switch (ValidPhases)
			{
			case "NVapor-Liquid":
				_phaseRadBtnList[0].IsEnabled = false;
				_phaseRadBtnList[1].IsEnabled = true;
				break;
			case "Vapor-Liquid":
			case "Vapor-Liquid-LiquidCondenser":
			case "Vapor-Liquid-FreeWaterCondenser":
				_phaseRadBtnList[0].IsEnabled = true;
				_phaseRadBtnList[1].IsEnabled = true;
				break;
			case "Vapor-Liquid-Liquid":
				_phaseRadBtnList[0].IsEnabled = true;
				_phaseRadBtnList[1].IsEnabled = true;
				_phaseRadBtnList[2].IsEnabled = true;
				_phaseRadBtnList[3].IsEnabled = true;
				break;
			case "Vapor-Liquid-FreeWater":
				_phaseRadBtnList[0].IsEnabled = true;
				_phaseRadBtnList[1].IsEnabled = true;
				_phaseRadBtnList[2].IsEnabled = true;
				break;
			default:
				_phaseRadBtnList[0].IsEnabled = true;
				_phaseRadBtnList[1].IsEnabled = true;
				break;
			}
		}

		private int GetNStage()
		{
			int result = 0;
			object nodeCxs = _nodeCxs;
			CxsNode val = (CxsNode)((nodeCxs is CxsNode) ? nodeCxs : null);
			if (val != null)
			{
				CxsCase val2 = val.Case;
				string text = val.Path + ".Input.NSTAGE";
				if (val2.BatchGet(text, "value") != null)
				{
					result = (int)val2.BatchGet(text, "value");
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		private string GetBlockID()
		{
			string result = "";
			object nodeCxs = _nodeCxs;
			CxsNode val = (CxsNode)((nodeCxs is CxsNode) ? nodeCxs : null);
			if (val != null)
			{
				_ = val.Case;
				string path = val.Path;
				path = path.TrimEnd('.');
				try
				{
					foreach (string item in new List<string> { "Top.appModelV8.", "Blocks.", "Data.", "Controllers." })
					{
						path = path.Replace(item, "");
					}
					result = path;
				}
				catch
				{
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		private void InitCurveList()
		{
			object nodeCxs = _nodeCxs;
			CxsNode val = (CxsNode)((nodeCxs is CxsNode) ? nodeCxs : null);
			if (val == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			CxsCase val2 = val.Case;
			string path = val.Path;
			int length = path.IndexOf("Blocks", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
			string text = path.Substring(0, length);
			text += "Components.Specifications.Input.TYPE";
			object obj = val2.BatchGet(text, "child_ids");
			if (obj != null)
			{
				List<string> list = (from x in obj.ToString().Split()
					where !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(x)
					select x).ToList();
				if (list.Count > 0)
				{
					CurveList.IsCheckAll = false;
					CurveList.SetCurves(list.ToArray());
				}
			}
		}

		private void InitDialogSettings()
		{
			object nodeCxs = _nodeCxs;
			CxsNode val = (CxsNode)((nodeCxs is CxsNode) ? nodeCxs : null);
			if (val == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			CxsCase val2 = val.Case;
			string text = val.Path + ".Input.VALIDPHASES";
			if (val2.BatchGet(text, "value") != null)
			{
				string key = (string)val2.BatchGet(text, "value");
				string faseRadBtns = "";
				if (sDicValidFases.ContainsKey(key))
				{
					faseRadBtns = sDicValidFases[key];
				}
				SetFaseRadBtns(faseRadBtns);
			}
		}

		void IRibbonPlotWizardType.ShowInputDialog()
		{
			//IL_0017: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_001d: Expected O, but got Unknown
			InitCurveList();
			InitDialogSettings();
			_currentConfigValue = string.Empty;
			PlotWizardTemplate val = new PlotWizardTemplate();
			((FrameworkElement)(object)val).MinWidth = 400.0;
			((ContentControl)(object)val).Content = this;
			MMHelp.SetHelpFile((DependencyObject)(object)val, _help.GetHelpFileName());
			MMHelp.SetHelpId((DependencyObject)(object)val, (int?)(int)_help.GetHelpID());
			((FrameworkElement)(object)val).Cursor = Cursors.Arrow;
			radBtnLiquid.IsChecked = true;
			try
			{
				((Window)(object)val).ShowDialog();
			}
			catch (Exception e)
			{
				LogAndDisplayErrorMessage(e, bDisplayMessageOnException: true);
				((Window)(object)val).Close();
			}
			((ContentControl)(object)val).Content = null;
		}

		private void LogAndDisplayErrorMessage(Exception e, bool bDisplayMessageOnException)
		{
			//IL_007a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			string text = string.Format("Unhandled Exception{1}Exception Message: {0}{1}Excetion Source: {2}{1}StackTrace: {3}", e.Message, Environment.NewLine, e.Source, e.StackTrace);
			SystemEnvironment.Logger.Log(text, (Category)1, (Priority)1);
			ILoggerFacade logger = SystemEnvironment.Logger;
			DebugLogger val = (DebugLogger)(object)((logger is DebugLogger) ? logger : null);
			if (bDisplayMessageOnException && val != null && val.FilePath != null)
			{
				MMMessageBox.Show(string.Format(ApwnResources.PlotUnhandledException, val.FilePath), ApwnResources.AspenPlus, (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)16, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
			}
		}

		void IRibbonPlotWizardType.Context(object HNode, ref object HWizardPlot, ref int ierror)
		{
			if (HWizardPlot == null || HNode == null)
			{
				ierror = 1;
				return;
			}
			_context = HNode as IHNode;
			_wizardPlot = HWizardPlot as IHWizardPlot;
			if (1 == 0)
			{
				ierror = 1;
				return;
			}
			GetNStage();
			GetBlockID();
			switch ("")
			{
			case "Vapor-Liquid":
			case "Vapor-Liquid-LiquidCondenser":
			case "Vapor-Liquid-FreeWaterCondenser":
				_phaseRadBtnList[0].IsEnabled = true;
				_phaseRadBtnList[1].IsEnabled = true;
				break;
			case "Vapor-Liquid-Liquid":
				_phaseRadBtnList[0].IsEnabled = true;
				_phaseRadBtnList[1].IsEnabled = true;
				_phaseRadBtnList[2].IsEnabled = true;
				_phaseRadBtnList[3].IsEnabled = true;
				break;
			case "Vapor-Liquid-FreeWater":
				_phaseRadBtnList[0].IsEnabled = true;
				_phaseRadBtnList[1].IsEnabled = true;
				_phaseRadBtnList[2].IsEnabled = true;
				break;
			case "NVapor-Liquid":
				_phaseRadBtnList[0].IsEnabled = false;
				_phaseRadBtnList[1].IsEnabled = true;
				break;
			default:
				_phaseRadBtnList[0].IsEnabled = true;
				_phaseRadBtnList[1].IsEnabled = true;
				break;
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_currentConfigValue))
			{
				return;
			}
			_basisRadBtnList[0].IsChecked = true;
			for (int i = 0; i < _phaseRadBtnList.Count(); i++)
			{
				if (_phaseRadBtnList[i].IsEnabled)
				{
					_phaseRadBtnList[i].IsChecked = true;
					break;
				}
			}
			CurveList.IsCheckAll = false;
		}

		public void UpdateWizardData()
		{
			int num = -1;
			string sTitle = string.Empty;
			for (int i = 0; i < _phaseRadBtnList.Count; i++)
			{
				if (_phaseRadBtnList[i].IsChecked == true)
				{
					num = i;
					sTitle = _strTitle[i];
					break;
				}
			}
			_sTitle = sTitle;
			int num2 = -1;
			string sTitleID = string.Empty;
			for (int j = 0; j < _basisRadBtnList.Count; j++)
			{
				if (_basisRadBtnList[j].IsChecked == true)
				{
					num2 = j;
					sTitleID = _strTBasis[j];
					break;
				}
			}
			_sTitleID = sTitleID;
			string text = _varNames[num2, num];
			if (text != "")
			{
				List<string> list = new List<string>(CurveList.GetSelectedCurves());
				int count = list.Count;
				_VarName = text;
				_selectedCurves.Clear();
				for (int k = 0; k < count; k++)
				{
					_selectedCurves.Add(list[k]);
				}
			}
		}

		private string GetTimeConfig()
		{
			string empty = string.Empty;
			string text = string.Empty;
			string text2 = string.Empty;
			_sTimeConfig = string.Empty;
			_sYAxisName = string.Empty;
			int count = _selectedCurves.Count;
			int nStage = GetNStage();
			string blockID = GetBlockID();
			string text3 = "";
			int num = 0;
			if (count > 0 && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_VarName) && nStage > 0 && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(blockID))
			{
				_sTimeConfig = _sTimePlot;
				for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
				{
					string text4 = _selectedCurves[i];
					string text5 = _VarName + "(*," + text4 + ")";
					text = text + "$" + text5;
					_sTimeConfig = _sTimeConfig + "!" + text5;
					text2 = text2 + "$" + sDicVarNameToPrefix[_VarName] + "(" + text4 + ")";
					_sYAxisName = _sYAxisName + "!" + text4;
				}
				_sYAxisName = _sYAxisName.Remove(0, 1);
				_sTitle = _sTitle.Replace("%ID%", blockID);
				_sTitle = _sTitle.Replace("%TID%", _sTitleID);
				text3 = _sTitle;
				num = text3.IndexOf(':');
				text3 = text3.Substring(num + 1);
				empty = empty + "Type:$" + _sTimePlot;
				empty = empty + "!VarPaths:" + text;
				empty = empty + "!nStage:$" + nStage;
				empty = empty + "!PlotTitle:$" + text3;
				empty = empty + "!YAxisNames:" + text2;
				empty = empty + "!BlockPath:$" + blockID;
				empty = empty + "!TabTitle:$" + _sTitle;
				empty += "!PlotSettings:$<Scpc ShowMultipleYAxes=\"false\">\r\n\t                    <OtherSettings>\r\n\t\t                    <ShowTitle>true</ShowTitle>\r\n\t\t                    <ShowLegend>true</ShowLegend>\r\n\t                    </OtherSettings>\r\n                    </Scpc>";
				_sTimeConfig = empty;
			}
			return _sTimeConfig;
		}

		private void ExecuteOKCommand(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			UpdateWizardData();
			_currentConfigValue = GetTimeConfig();
			_help.CloseForm((UserControl)this, (bool?)true);
		}

		private void CanExecuteOKCommand(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			int num = CurveList.GetSelectedCurves().Count();
			if (num == CurveList.Curves.Count)
			{
				CurveList.IsCheckAll = true;
			}
			else
			{
				string[] selectedCurves = CurveList.GetSelectedCurves();
				CurveList.IsCheckAll = false;
				CurveList.SetSelectedCurves(selectedCurves);
			}
			e.CanExecute = num != 0;
		}

		private void ExecuteCancelCommand(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			_currentConfigValue = string.Empty;
			_sYAxisName = string.Empty;
			_sTitle = string.Empty;
			_help.CloseForm((UserControl)this, (bool?)false);
		}

		private void SetVariables()
		{
			_varNames = new string[2, 4]
			{
				{ "Y", "X", "X1", "X2" },
				{ "YM", "XM", "XM1", "XM2" }
			};
			_strTitle = new string[4]
			{
				WpfPLBatchSepResources.PotComposition_Title_Vapor,
				WpfPLBatchSepResources.PotComposition_Title_Liquid,
				WpfPLBatchSepResources.PotComposition_Title_1stLiquid,
				WpfPLBatchSepResources.PotComposition_Title_2ndLiquid
			};
			_strTBasis = new string[2]
			{
				WpfPLBatchSepResources.Mole,
				WpfPLBatchSepResources.Mass
			};
		}

		private void SetControls()
		{
			_phaseRadBtnList = new List<RadioButton>();
			_phaseRadBtnList.Add(radBtnVapor);
			_phaseRadBtnList.Add(radBtnLiquid);
			_phaseRadBtnList.Add(radBtn1stLiquid);
			_phaseRadBtnList.Add(radBtn2ndLiquid);
			for (int i = 0; i < _phaseRadBtnList.Count; i++)
			{
				_phaseRadBtnList[i].IsEnabled = false;
				_phaseRadBtnList[i].IsChecked = false;
			}
			_basisRadBtnList = new List<RadioButton>();
			_basisRadBtnList.Add(radBtnMole);
			_basisRadBtnList.Add(radBtnMass);
		}

		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "4.0.0.0")]
		public void InitializeComponent()
		{
			if (!_contentLoaded)
			{
				_contentLoaded = true;
				Uri resourceLocator = new Uri("/WpfPLBatchSep;component/potcomposition.xaml", UriKind.Relative);
				Application.LoadComponent(this, resourceLocator);
			}
		}

		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "4.0.0.0")]
		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		void IComponentConnector.Connect(int connectionId, object target)
		{
			switch (connectionId)
			{
			case 1:
				radBtnVapor = (RadioButton)target;
				break;
			case 2:
				radBtnLiquid = (RadioButton)target;
				break;
			case 3:
				radBtn1stLiquid = (RadioButton)target;
				break;
			case 4:
				radBtn2ndLiquid = (RadioButton)target;
				break;
			case 5:
				radBtnMole = (RadioButton)target;
				break;
			case 6:
				radBtnMass = (RadioButton)target;
				break;
			case 7:
				CurveList = (CurvesSelectorControl)target;
				break;
			default:
				_contentLoaded = true;
				break;
			}
		}
	}

		private void GetConfigString()
		{
			int nStage = GetNStage();
			string blockID = GetBlockID();
			_sYAxisName = WpfPLBatchSepResources.PotDuty;
			_sTitle = WpfPLBatchSepResources.BlockTitle + WpfPLBatchSepResources.PotDuty;

			text = text + "!PlotTitle:$" + sTitle;

		private int GetNStage()
		{
			int result = 0;
			object nodeCxs = _nodeCxs;
			CxsNode val = (CxsNode)((nodeCxs is CxsNode) ? nodeCxs : null);
			if (val != null)
			{
				CxsCase val2 = val.Case;
				string text = val.Path + ".Input.NSTAGE";
				if (val2.BatchGet(text, "value") != null)
				{
					result = (int)val2.BatchGet(text, "value");
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		private string GetBlockID()
		{
			string result = "";
			object nodeCxs = _nodeCxs;
			CxsNode val = (CxsNode)((nodeCxs is CxsNode) ? nodeCxs : null);
			if (val != null)
			{
				_ = val.Case;
				string path = val.Path;
				path = path.TrimEnd('.');
				try
				{
					foreach (string item in new List<string> { "Top.appModelV8.", "Blocks.", "Data.", "Controllers." })
					{
						path = path.Replace(item, "");
					}
					result = path;
				}
				catch
				{
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		private void SetFaseRadBtns(string ValidPhases)
		{
			switch (ValidPhases)
			{
			case "Vapor-Liquid":
			case "Vapor-Liquid-LiquidCondenser":
			case "Vapor-Liquid-FreeWaterCondenser":
				_phaseRadBtnList[0].IsEnabled = true;
				_phaseRadBtnList[1].IsEnabled = true;
				break;
			case "Vapor-Liquid-Liquid":
				_phaseRadBtnList[0].IsEnabled = true;
				_phaseRadBtnList[1].IsEnabled = true;
				_phaseRadBtnList[2].IsEnabled = true;
				_phaseRadBtnList[3].IsEnabled = true;
				break;
			case "Vapor-Liquid-FreeWater":
				_phaseRadBtnList[0].IsEnabled = true;
				_phaseRadBtnList[1].IsEnabled = true;
				_phaseRadBtnList[2].IsEnabled = true;
				break;
			default:
				_phaseRadBtnList[0].IsEnabled = true;
				_phaseRadBtnList[1].IsEnabled = true;
				break;
			}
		}

		private void InitCurveList()
		{
			object nodeCxs = _nodeCxs;
			CxsNode val = (CxsNode)((nodeCxs is CxsNode) ? nodeCxs : null);
			if (val == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			CxsCase val2 = val.Case;
			string path = val.Path;
			int length = path.IndexOf("Blocks", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
			string text = path.Substring(0, length);
			text += "Components.Specifications.Input.TYPE";
			object obj = val2.BatchGet(text, "child_ids");
			if (obj != null)
			{
				List<string> list = (from x in obj.ToString().Split()
					where !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(x)
					select x).ToList();
				if (list.Count > 0)
				{
					list.Add(_sTotal);
					CurveList.IsCheckAll = false;
					CurveList.SetCurves(list.ToArray());
				}
			}
		}

		private void InitDialogSettings()
		{
			object nodeCxs = _nodeCxs;
			CxsNode val = (CxsNode)((nodeCxs is CxsNode) ? nodeCxs : null);
			if (val == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			CxsCase val2 = val.Case;
			string text = val.Path + ".Input.VALIDPHASES";
			if (val2.BatchGet(text, "value") != null)
			{
				string key = (string)val2.BatchGet(text, "value");
				string faseRadBtns = "";
				if (sDicValidFases.ContainsKey(key))
				{
					faseRadBtns = sDicValidFases[key];
				}
				SetFaseRadBtns(faseRadBtns);
			}
		}

		void IRibbonPlotWizardType.ShowInputDialog()
		{
			//IL_0017: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_001d: Expected O, but got Unknown
			InitCurveList();
			InitDialogSettings();
			_currentConfigValue = string.Empty;
			PlotWizardTemplate val = new PlotWizardTemplate();
			((FrameworkElement)(object)val).MinWidth = 400.0;
			((ContentControl)(object)val).Content = this;
			MMHelp.SetHelpFile((DependencyObject)(object)val, _help.GetHelpFileName());
			MMHelp.SetHelpId((DependencyObject)(object)val, (int?)(int)_help.GetHelpID());
			((FrameworkElement)(object)val).Cursor = Cursors.Arrow;
			radBtnLiquid.IsChecked = true;
			try
			{
				((Window)(object)val).ShowDialog();
			}
			catch (Exception e)
			{
				LogAndDisplayErrorMessage(e, bDisplayMessageOnException: true);
				((Window)(object)val).Close();
			}
			((ContentControl)(object)val).Content = null;
		}

		private void LogAndDisplayErrorMessage(Exception e, bool bDisplayMessageOnException)
		{
			//IL_007a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			string text = string.Format("Unhandled Exception{1}Exception Message: {0}{1}Excetion Source: {2}{1}StackTrace: {3}", e.Message, Environment.NewLine, e.Source, e.StackTrace);
			SystemEnvironment.Logger.Log(text, (Category)1, (Priority)1);
			ILoggerFacade logger = SystemEnvironment.Logger;
			DebugLogger val = (DebugLogger)(object)((logger is DebugLogger) ? logger : null);
			if (bDisplayMessageOnException && val != null && val.FilePath != null)
			{
				MMMessageBox.Show(string.Format(ApwnResources.PlotUnhandledException, val.FilePath), ApwnResources.AspenPlus, (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)16, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
			}
		}

		void IRibbonPlotWizardType.Context(object HNode, ref object HWizardPlot, ref int ierror)
		{
			if (HWizardPlot == null || HNode == null)
			{
				ierror = 1;
				return;
			}
			_context = HNode as IHNode;
			_wizardPlot = HWizardPlot as IHWizardPlot;
			if (1 == 0)
			{
				ierror = 1;
				return;
			}
			string text = "";
			GetNStage();
			GetBlockID();
			switch (text)
			{
			case "Vapor-Liquid":
			case "Vapor-Liquid-LiquidCondenser":
			case "Vapor-Liquid-FreeWaterCondenser":
				_phaseRadBtnList[0].IsEnabled = true;
				_phaseRadBtnList[1].IsEnabled = true;
				break;
			case "Vapor-Liquid-Liquid":
				_phaseRadBtnList[0].IsEnabled = true;
				_phaseRadBtnList[1].IsEnabled = true;
				_phaseRadBtnList[2].IsEnabled = true;
				_phaseRadBtnList[3].IsEnabled = true;
				break;
			case "Vapor-Liquid-FreeWater":
				_phaseRadBtnList[0].IsEnabled = true;
				_phaseRadBtnList[1].IsEnabled = true;
				_phaseRadBtnList[2].IsEnabled = true;
				break;
			default:
				_phaseRadBtnList[0].IsEnabled = true;
				_phaseRadBtnList[1].IsEnabled = true;
				break;
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_currentConfigValue))
			{
				return;
			}
			_basisRadBtnList[0].IsChecked = true;
			for (int i = 0; i < _phaseRadBtnList.Count(); i++)
			{
				if (_phaseRadBtnList[i].IsEnabled)
				{
					_phaseRadBtnList[i].IsChecked = true;
					break;
				}
			}
		}

		public void UpdateWizardData()
		{
			int num = -1;
			string sTitle = string.Empty;
			for (int i = 0; i < _phaseRadBtnList.Count; i++)
			{
				if (_phaseRadBtnList[i].IsChecked == true)
				{
					num = i;
					sTitle = _strTitle[i];
					break;
				}
			}
			_sTitle = sTitle;
			int num2 = -1;
			string sYAxisName = string.Empty;
			for (int j = 0; j < _basisRadBtnList.Count; j++)
			{
				if (_basisRadBtnList[j].IsChecked == true)
				{
					num2 = j;
					sYAxisName = _strYAxisTitle[j];
					break;
				}
			}
			_sYAxisName = sYAxisName;
			string text = _varNames[num2, num];
			_VarName = string.Empty;
			if (text != "")
			{
				List<string> list = new List<string>(CurveList.GetSelectedCurves());
				int count = list.Count;
				_VarName = text;
				_selectedCurves.Clear();
				for (int k = 0; k < count; k++)
				{
					_selectedCurves.Add(list[k]);
				}
			}
		}

		private string GetTimeConfig()
		{
			string empty = string.Empty;
			string text = "";
			int num = 0;
			string text2 = string.Empty;
			string text3 = string.Empty;
			_sTimeConfig = string.Empty;
			string text4 = "Mole";
			string text5 = "Mass";
			string text6 = "Total Moles";
			string text7 = "Total Mass";
			string text8 = "POT_MC";
			string text9 = "POT_MMC";
			string text10 = "";
			string text11 = "";
			for (int i = 0; i < _basisRadBtnList.Count; i++)
			{
				if (_basisRadBtnList[i].IsChecked == true)
				{
					_sTitleID = _strTBasis[i];
					break;
				}
			}
			int count = _selectedCurves.Count;
			int nStage = GetNStage();
			string blockID = GetBlockID();
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_VarName) && nStage > 0 && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(blockID))
			{
				_sTimeConfig = _sTimePlot;
				if (count == 0 || _sTitleID.Equals("Volume"))
				{
					string text12 = "POT_" + _VarName;
					_sTimeConfig = _sTimeConfig + "!" + text12;
					_sTitle = _sTitle.Replace("%ID%", blockID);
					_sTitle = _sTitle.Replace("%TID%", "");
					_sTitle = _sTitle.Replace("%BASIS%", _sYAxisName);
					text = _sTitle;
					num = text.IndexOf(':');
					text = text.Substring(num + 1);
					text = text.Replace("Holdup ", "");
					empty = empty + "Type:$" + _sTimePlot;
					empty = empty + "!VarPaths:$" + text12;
					empty = empty + "!nStage:$" + nStage;
					empty = empty + "!PlotTitle:$" + text;
					empty = empty + "!YAxisNames:$" + text;
					empty = empty + "!BlockPath:$" + blockID;
					_sTitle = _sTitle.Replace("Holdup ", "");
					empty = empty + "!TabTitle:$" + _sTitle;
					empty += "!PlotSettings:$<Scpc ShowMultipleYAxes=\"true\">\r\n\t                    <OtherSettings>\r\n\t\t                    <ShowTitle>true</ShowTitle>\r\n\t\t                    <ShowLegend>false</ShowLegend>\r\n\t                    </OtherSettings>\r\n                    </Scpc>";
					_sTimeConfig = empty;
				}
				else
				{
					if (_sTitleID == WpfPLBatchSepResources.Mole)
					{
						text10 = text4;
						text11 = text6;
					}
					if (_sTitleID == WpfPLBatchSepResources.Mass)
					{
						text10 = text5;
						text11 = text7;
					}
					for (int j = 0; j < count; j++)
					{
						string text13 = _selectedCurves[j];
						string text12;
						if (text13 == _sTotal)
						{
							text12 = "POT_" + _VarName;
							text2 = text2 + "$" + text12;
							text3 = text3 + "$" + text11;
							continue;
						}
						text12 = "POT_" + _VarName + "C(" + text13 + ")";
						text2 = text2 + "$" + text12;
						_sTimeConfig = _sTimeConfig + "!" + text12;
						text3 = text3 + "$" + text10 + "(" + text13 + ")";
					}
					_sTitle = _sTitle.Replace("%BASIS%", text10);
					_sTitle = _sTitle.Replace("%ID%", blockID);
					_sTitle = _sTitle.Replace("%TID%", "");
					text = _sTitle;
					num = text.IndexOf(':');
					text = text.Substring(num + 1);
					empty = empty + "Type:$" + _sTimePlot;
					empty = empty + "!VarPaths:" + text2;
					empty = empty + "!nStage:$" + nStage;
					empty = empty + "!PlotTitle:$" + text;
					empty = empty + "!YAxisNames:" + text3;
					empty = empty + "!BlockPath:$" + blockID;
					empty = empty + "!TabTitle:$" + _sTitle;
					empty += "!PlotSettings:$<Scpc ShowMultipleYAxes=\"false\">\r\n\t                    <OtherSettings>\r\n\t\t                    <ShowTitle>true</ShowTitle>\r\n\t\t                    <ShowLegend>true</ShowLegend>\r\n\t                    </OtherSettings>\r\n                    </Scpc>";
					_sTimeConfig = empty;
				}
			}
			return _sTimeConfig;
		}

		private void ExecuteOKCommand(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			UpdateWizardData();
			_currentConfigValue = GetTimeConfig();
			_help.CloseForm((UserControl)this, (bool?)true);
		}

		private void CanExecuteOKCommand(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < _basisRadBtnList.Count; i++)
			{
				if (_basisRadBtnList[i] == radBtnVol && _basisRadBtnList[i].IsChecked == true)
				{
					CurveList.IsEnabled = false;
					if (CurveList.GetSelectedCurves().Count() == CurveList.Curves.Count)
					{
						CurveList.IsCheckAll = true;
					}
					else
					{
						string[] selectedCurves = CurveList.GetSelectedCurves();
						CurveList.IsCheckAll = false;
						CurveList.SetSelectedCurves(selectedCurves);
					}
					e.CanExecute = true;
					continue;
				}
				CurveList.IsEnabled = true;
				int num = CurveList.GetSelectedCurves().Count();
				if (num == CurveList.Curves.Count)
				{
					CurveList.IsCheckAll = true;
				}
				else
				{
					string[] selectedCurves2 = CurveList.GetSelectedCurves();
					CurveList.IsCheckAll = false;
					CurveList.SetSelectedCurves(selectedCurves2);
				}
				e.CanExecute = num != 0;
			}
		}

		private void ExecuteCancelCommand(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			_sTitle = string.Empty;
			_sYAxisName = string.Empty;
			_currentConfigValue = string.Empty;
			_help.CloseForm((UserControl)this, (bool?)false);
		}

		private void SetVariables()
		{
			_varNames = new string[3, 4]
			{
				{ "MV", "ML", "ML1", "ML2" },
				{ "MMV", "MML", "MML1", "MML2" },
				{ "VV", "VL", "VL1", "VL2" }
			};
			_strTBasis = new string[3]
			{
				WpfPLBatchSepResources.Mole,
				WpfPLBatchSepResources.Mass,
				WpfPLBatchSepResources.Volume
			};
			_strTitle = new string[4]
			{
				WpfPLBatchSepResources.PotHoldup_Title_Vapor,
				WpfPLBatchSepResources.PotHoldup_Title_Liquid,
				WpfPLBatchSepResources.PotHoldup_Title_1stLiquid,
				WpfPLBatchSepResources.PotHoldup_Title_2ndLiquid
			};
			_strYAxisTitle = new string[3]
			{
				WpfPLBatchSepResources.Moles,
				WpfPLBatchSepResources.Mass,
				WpfPLBatchSepResources.Volume
			};
		}

		private void SetControls()
		{
			_phaseRadBtnList = new List<RadioButton>();
			_phaseRadBtnList.Add(radBtnVapor);
			_phaseRadBtnList.Add(radBtnLiquid);
			_phaseRadBtnList.Add(radBtn1stLiquid);
			_phaseRadBtnList.Add(radBtn2ndLiquid);
			for (int i = 0; i < _phaseRadBtnList.Count; i++)
			{
				_phaseRadBtnList[i].IsEnabled = false;
				_phaseRadBtnList[i].IsChecked = false;
			}
			_basisRadBtnList = new List<RadioButton>();
			_basisRadBtnList.Add(radBtnMole);
			_basisRadBtnList.Add(radBtnMass);
			_basisRadBtnList.Add(radBtnVol);

		[DebuggerNonUserCode]

		private void InitCurveList()
		{
			object nodeCxs = _nodeCxs;
			CxsNode val = (CxsNode)((nodeCxs is CxsNode) ? nodeCxs : null);
			if (val == null)
			{
				return;

			CxsCase val2 = val.Case;
			string path = val.Path;
			int length = path.IndexOf("Blocks", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
			string text = path.Substring(0, length);
			text += "Components.Specifications.Input.TYPE";
			object obj = val2.BatchGet(text, "child_ids");
			if (obj != null)
			{
				List<string> list = (from x in obj.ToString().Split()
					where !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(x)
					select x).ToList();
				if (list.Count > 0)
				{
					CurveList.IsCheckAll = false;
					CurveList.SetCurves(list.ToArray());
				}
			}
		}

		private void LogAndDisplayErrorMessage(Exception e, bool bDisplayMessageOnException)

			ILoggerFacade logger = SystemEnvironment.Logger;
			DebugLogger val = (DebugLogger)(object)((logger is DebugLogger) ? logger : null);
			if (bDisplayMessageOnException && val != null && val.FilePath != null)
			{
				MMMessageBox.Show(string.Format(ApwnResources.PlotUnhandledException, val.FilePath), ApwnResources.AspenPlus, (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)16, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);

		public void UpdateWizardData()
		{
			_sTitle = _strTitle[0];
			string text = _varNames[0];
			if (text != "")
			{
				List<string> list = new List<string>(CurveList.GetSelectedCurves());
				int count = list.Count;
				_VarName = text;
				_selectedCurves.Clear();
				for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
				{
					_selectedCurves.Add(list[i]);
				}
			}
		}

		private string GetTimeConfig()
		{
			string empty = string.Empty;
			string text = string.Empty;
			string text2 = string.Empty;
			_sTimeConfig = string.Empty;
			_sYAxisName = string.Empty;
			int count = _selectedCurves.Count;
			int nStage = GetNStage();
			string blockID = GetBlockID();
			if (count > 0 && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_VarName) && nStage > 0 && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(blockID))
			{
				_sTimeConfig = _sTimePlot;
				for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
				{
					string text3 = _selectedCurves[i];
					string text4 = _VarName + "(*," + text3 + ")";
					text = text + "$" + text4;
					_sTimeConfig = _sTimeConfig + "!" + text4;
					text2 = text2 + "$" + text3;
					_sYAxisName = _sYAxisName + "!" + text3;
				}
				_sYAxisName = _sYAxisName.Remove(0, 1);
				_sTitle = _sTitle.Replace("%ID%", blockID);
				string sTitle = _sTitle;
				int num = sTitle.IndexOf(':');
				sTitle = sTitle.Substring(num + 1);
				empty = empty + "Type:$" + _sTimePlot;
				empty = empty + "!VarPaths:" + text;
				empty = empty + "!nStage:$" + nStage;
				empty = empty + "!PlotTitle:$" + sTitle;
				empty = empty + "!YAxisNames:" + text2;
				empty = empty + "!BlockPath:$" + blockID;
				empty = empty + "!TabTitle:$" + _sTitle;
				empty += "!PlotSettings:$<Scpc ShowMultipleYAxes=\"false\">\r\n\t                    <OtherSettings>\r\n\t\t                    <ShowTitle>true</ShowTitle>\r\n\t\t                    <ShowLegend>true</ShowLegend>\r\n\t                    </OtherSettings>\r\n                    </Scpc>";
				_sTimeConfig = empty;
			}
			return _sTimeConfig;
		}

		private void ExecuteOKCommand(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			UpdateWizardData();
			_currentConfigValue = GetTimeConfig();
			_help.CloseForm((UserControl)this, (bool?)true);
		}

		private void CanExecuteOKCommand(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			int num = CurveList.GetSelectedCurves().Count();
			if (num == CurveList.Curves.Count)
			{
				CurveList.IsCheckAll = true;
			}
			else
			{
				string[] selectedCurves = CurveList.GetSelectedCurves();
				CurveList.IsCheckAll = false;
				CurveList.SetSelectedCurves(selectedCurves);
			}
			e.CanExecute = num != 0;
		}

		private void ExecuteCancelCommand(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			_currentConfigValue = string.Empty;
			_sYAxisName = string.Empty;
			_sTitle = string.Empty;
			_help.CloseForm((UserControl)this, (bool?)false);
		}

		private void SetVariables()
		{
			_varNames = new string[1] { "RATE" };
			_strTitle = new string[1] { WpfPLBatchSepResources.PotReactionRates_Title };
		}

		private void SetControls()
		{
		}

		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "4.0.0.0")]
		public void InitializeComponent()
		{
			if (!_contentLoaded)
			{
				_contentLoaded = true;
				Uri resourceLocator = new Uri("/WpfPLBatchSep;component/potreactionrates.xaml", UriKind.Relative);
				Application.LoadComponent(this, resourceLocator);
			}
		}

		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "4.0.0.0")]
		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		void IComponentConnector.Connect(int connectionId, object target)
		{
			if (connectionId == 1)
			{
				CurveList = (CurvesSelectorControl)target;
			}
			else
			{
				_contentLoaded = true;
			}
		}
	}

		void IRibbonPlotWizardType.GetFieldStyle(string sFieldName, ref string sCurrentVal)
		{
			if (sFieldName.CompareTo(_stageOrderFieldId) == 0)
			{
				if (_wizardPlot.PlotVal.ReverseXAxis)
				{
					sCurrentVal = _descending;
				}
				else
				{
					sCurrentVal = _ascending;
				}
			}
			else if (sFieldName.CompareTo(_stageOnFieldId) == 0)
			{
				if (_wizardPlot.PlotVal.SwapAxises)
				{
					sCurrentVal = _yAxis;
				}
				else
				{
					sCurrentVal = _xAxis;
				}
			}
		}

		void IRibbonPlotWizardType.SetFieldStyle(string sFieldName, string sValue)
		{
			if (sFieldName.CompareTo(_stageOrderFieldId) == 0)
			{
				if (sValue.CompareTo(_ascending) == 0)
				{
					_wizardPlot.PlotVal.ReverseXAxis = false;
				}
				else
				{
					_wizardPlot.PlotVal.ReverseXAxis = true;
				}
			}
			else if (sFieldName.CompareTo(_stageOnFieldId) == 0)
			{
				if (sValue.CompareTo(_yAxis) == 0)
				{
					_wizardPlot.PlotVal.SwapAxises = true;
				}
				else
				{
					_wizardPlot.PlotVal.SwapAxises = false;
				}
			}
		}

		public void UpdateWizardData()
		{
			int nStage = GetNStage();
			double[] array = new double[nStage];
			for (int i = 0; i < nStage; i++)
			{
				array[i] = 0.0;
			}
			if (_mvarControlContext != null)
			{
				if (_CurrentPresUnit == "")
				{
					_CurrentPresUnit = _mvarControlContext.Pot.P.Units("CurrentUnits");
				}
				array[0] = _mvarControlContext.Condenser.P.value(_CurrentPresUnit);
				for (int j = 2; j < nStage; j++)
				{
					array[j - 1] = _mvarControlContext.stage(j).P.value(_CurrentPresUnit);
				}
				array[nStage - 1] = _mvarControlContext.Pot.P.value(_CurrentPresUnit);
			}
			_wizardPlot.RemoveAllDependentVariable();
			_wizardPlot.PlotVal.NVariables = 1;
			_wizardPlot.PlotVal.NXVariables = 1;
			_wizardPlot.PlotVal.NPoints = nStage;
			string text = "Pressure  " + _CurrentPresUnit;
			((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_Label(0, text);
			((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_YAxisTitle(Type.Missing, text);
			for (int k = 0; k < nStage; k++)
			{
				double num = k + 1;
				((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_XData(0, k, (short)0, (object)num);
				((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_YData(0, k, (short)0, (object)array[k]);
			}
			_help.SaveUnitsComboText(_context.Application.Tree, _wizardPlot, _CurrentPresUnit);
		}

		private void SetVariables()
		{
			_VarNames = new string[1] { "Pot.P" };
		}

				_contentLoaded = true;

				Application.LoadComponent(this, resourceLocator);
			}

		private int GetNStage()
		{
			int result = 0;
			object nodeCxs = _nodeCxs;
			CxsNode val = (CxsNode)((nodeCxs is CxsNode) ? nodeCxs : null);
			if (val != null)
			{
				CxsCase val2 = val.Case;
				string text = val.Path + ".Input.NSTAGE";
				if (val2.BatchGet(text, "value") != null)
				{
					result = (int)val2.BatchGet(text, "value");
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		private string GetBlockID()
		{
			string result = "";
			object nodeCxs = _nodeCxs;
			CxsNode val = (CxsNode)((nodeCxs is CxsNode) ? nodeCxs : null);
			if (val != null)
			{
				_ = val.Case;
				string path = val.Path;
				path = path.TrimEnd('.');
				try
				{
					foreach (string item in new List<string> { "Top.appModelV8.", "Blocks.", "Data.", "Controllers." })
					{
						path = path.Replace(item, "");
					}
					result = path;
				}
				catch
				{
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		private void InitCurveList()
		{
			object nodeCxs = _nodeCxs;
			CxsNode val = (CxsNode)((nodeCxs is CxsNode) ? nodeCxs : null);
			if (val == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			CxsCase val2 = val.Case;
			string path = val.Path;
			int length = path.IndexOf("Blocks", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
			string text = path.Substring(0, length);
			text += "Components.Specifications.Input.TYPE";
			object obj = val2.BatchGet(text, "child_ids");
			if (obj != null)
			{
				List<string> list = (from x in obj.ToString().Split()
					where !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(x)
					select x).ToList();
				if (list.Count > 0)
				{
					CurveList.IsCheckAll = false;
					CurveList.SetCurves(list.ToArray());
				}
			}
		}

		void IRibbonPlotWizardType.ShowInputDialog()
