			//IL_0017: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_001d: Expected O, but got Unknown
			InitCurveList();
			SetComboBox();
			_currentConfigValue = string.Empty;

		}

		private void LogAndDisplayErrorMessage(Exception e, bool bDisplayMessageOnException)

			//IL_007a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			string text = string.Format("Unhandled Exception{1}Exception Message: {0}{1}Excetion Source: {2}{1}StackTrace: {3}", e.Message, Environment.NewLine, e.Source, e.StackTrace);
			SystemEnvironment.Logger.Log(text, (Category)1, (Priority)1);
			ILoggerFacade logger = SystemEnvironment.Logger;
			DebugLogger val = (DebugLogger)(object)((logger is DebugLogger) ? logger : null);
			if (bDisplayMessageOnException && val != null && val.FilePath != null)
			{
				MMMessageBox.Show(string.Format(ApwnResources.PlotUnhandledException, val.FilePath), ApwnResources.AspenPlus, (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)16, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
			}
		}

		void IRibbonPlotWizardType.Context(object HNode, ref object HWizardPlot, ref int ierror)
		{
			if (HWizardPlot == null || HNode == null)
			{
				ierror = 1;
				return;
			}
			_context = HNode as IHNode;
			_wizardPlot = HWizardPlot as IHWizardPlot;
			if (1 == 0)
			{
				ierror = 1;
				return;
			}
			GetNStage();
			GetBlockID();
		}

		public void UpdateWizardData()
		{
			List<string> list = new List<string>(CurveList.GetSelectedCurves());
			int count = list.Count;
			_selectedCurves.Clear();
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				_selectedCurves.Add(list[i]);
			}
		}

		private string GetTimeConfig()
		{
			string varName = "DISTRECV(#)_Z(?)";
			string varName2 = "DISTRECV(#)_ZM(?)";
			string text = "z_mole";
			string text2 = "z_mass";
			string text3 = "";
			for (int i = 0; i < _basisRadBtnList.Count; i++)
			{
				if (_basisRadBtnList[i].IsChecked == true)
				{
					_sTitleID = _strTBasis[i];
					break;
				}
			}
			if (_sTitleID == "Mole")
			{
				text3 = text;
				_VarName = varName;
			}
			if (_sTitleID == "Mass")
			{
				text3 = text2;
				_VarName = varName2;
			}
			string empty = string.Empty;
			string text4 = string.Empty;
			string text5 = string.Empty;
			_sTimeConfig = string.Empty;
			_sYAxisName = string.Empty;
			int count = _selectedCurves.Count;
			int nStage = GetNStage();
			string blockID = GetBlockID();
			string text6 = "";
			int num = 0;
			if (count > 0 && nStage > 0 && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(blockID))
			{
				_sTimeConfig = _sTimePlot;
				for (int j = 0; j < count; j++)
				{
					string text7 = _selectedCurves[j];
					string varName3 = _VarName;
					varName3 = varName3.Replace("#", _sSelectedItem);
					varName3 = varName3.Replace("?", text7);
					text4 = text4 + "$" + varName3;
					_sTimeConfig = _sTimeConfig + "!" + varName3;
					text5 = text5 + "$" + text3 + "(" + text7 + ")";
				}
				_sTitle = WpfPLBatchSepResources.DistillateReceiver_Title;
				_sTitle = _sTitle.Replace("%ID%", blockID);
				_sTitle = _sTitle.Replace("#", _sSelectedItem);
				_sTitle = _sTitle.Replace("%TID%", _sTitleID);
				text6 = _sTitle;
				num = text6.IndexOf(':');
				text6 = text6.Substring(num + 1);
				empty = empty + "Type:$" + _sTimePlot;
				empty = empty + "!VarPaths:" + text4;
				empty = empty + "!nStage:$" + nStage;
				empty = empty + "!PlotTitle:$" + text6;
				empty = empty + "!YAxisNames:" + text5;
				empty = empty + "!BlockPath:$" + blockID;
				empty = empty + "!TabTitle:$" + _sTitle;
				empty += "!PlotSettings:$<Scpc ShowMultipleYAxes=\"false\">\r\n\t                    <OtherSettings>\r\n\t\t                    <ShowTitle>true</ShowTitle>\r\n\t\t                    <ShowLegend>true</ShowLegend>\r\n\t                    </OtherSettings>\r\n                    </Scpc>";
				_sTimeConfig = empty;
			}
			return _sTimeConfig;
		}

		private void ExecuteOKCommand(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			UpdateWizardData();
			_currentConfigValue = GetTimeConfig();
			_help.CloseForm((UserControl)this, (bool?)true);
		}

		private void CanExecuteOKCommand(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			int num = CurveList.GetSelectedCurves().Count();
			if (num == CurveList.Curves.Count)
			{
				CurveList.IsCheckAll = true;
			}
			else
			{
				string[] selectedCurves = CurveList.GetSelectedCurves();
				CurveList.IsCheckAll = false;
				CurveList.SetSelectedCurves(selectedCurves);
			}
			e.CanExecute = num != 0;
		}

		private void ExecuteCancelCommand(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			_currentConfigValue = string.Empty;
			_sYAxisName = string.Empty;
			_sTitle = string.Empty;
			_help.CloseForm((UserControl)this, (bool?)false);
		}

		private void SetVariables()
		{
			_strTBasis = new string[2]
			{
				WpfPLBatchSepResources.Mole,
				WpfPLBatchSepResources.Mass
			};
		}

		private void SetControls()
		{
			_basisRadBtnList = new List<RadioButton>();
			_basisRadBtnList.Add(radBtnMole);
			_basisRadBtnList.Add(radBtnMass);
			_basisRadBtnList[0].IsChecked = true;
		}

		private void SetComboBox()
		{
			_selComboIndex = 0;
			int num = 1;
			object nodeCxs = _nodeCxs;
			CxsNode val = (CxsNode)((nodeCxs is CxsNode) ? nodeCxs : null);
			if (val != null)
			{
				CxsCase val2 = val.Case;
				string text = val.Path + ".Input.NDISTRECV";
				if (val2.BatchGet(text, "value") != null)
				{
					num = (int)val2.BatchGet(text, "value");
				}
			}
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			for (int i = 1; i <= num; i++)
			{
				list.Add(i.ToString());
			}
			distReceiver.ItemsSource = list;
			distReceiver.SelectedIndex = _selComboIndex;
		}

		private void distReceiver_SelectionChanged(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			ComboBox comboBox = sender as ComboBox;
			_sSelectedItem = comboBox.SelectedItem as string;
			int result = 0;
			if (int.TryParse(_sSelectedItem, out result))
			{
				_selComboIndex = result - 1;
			}
			else
			{
				_selComboIndex = 0;
			}
		}

		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "4.0.0.0")]
		public void InitializeComponent()
		{
			if (!_contentLoaded)
			{
				_contentLoaded = true;
				Uri resourceLocator = new Uri("/WpfPLBatchSep;component/receivercomposition.xaml", UriKind.Relative);
				Application.LoadComponent(this, resourceLocator);
			}
		}

		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "4.0.0.0")]
		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		void IComponentConnector.Connect(int connectionId, object target)
		{
			switch (connectionId)
			{
			case 1:
				distReceiver = (ComboBox)target;
				distReceiver.SelectionChanged += distReceiver_SelectionChanged;
				break;
			case 2:
				radBtnMole = (RadioButton)target;
				break;
			case 3:
				radBtnMass = (RadioButton)target;
				break;
			case 4:
				CurveList = (CurvesSelectorControl)target;
				break;
			default:
				_contentLoaded = true;
				break;
			}
		}
	}

			string result = "";
			object nodeCxs = _nodeCxs;
			CxsNode val = (CxsNode)((nodeCxs is CxsNode) ? nodeCxs : null);
			if (val != null)
			{
				_ = val.Case;
				string path = val.Path;
				path = path.TrimEnd('.');
				try
				{
					foreach (string item in new List<string> { "Top.appModelV8.", "Blocks.", "Data.", "Controllers." })
					{
						path = path.Replace(item, "");
					}
					result = path;
				}
				catch
				{
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		private void InitCurveList()
		{
			object nodeCxs = _nodeCxs;
			CxsNode val = (CxsNode)((nodeCxs is CxsNode) ? nodeCxs : null);
			if (val == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			CxsCase val2 = val.Case;
			string path = val.Path;
			int length = path.IndexOf("Blocks", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
			string text = path.Substring(0, length);
			text += "Components.Specifications.Input.TYPE";
			object obj = val2.BatchGet(text, "child_ids");
			if (obj != null)
			{
				List<string> list = (from x in obj.ToString().Split()
					where !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(x)
					select x).ToList();
				if (list.Count > 0)
				{
					list.Add(_sTotal);
					CurveList.IsCheckAll = false;
					CurveList.SetCurves(list.ToArray());
				}
			}
		}

		}

		private void LogAndDisplayErrorMessage(Exception e, bool bDisplayMessageOnException)

			string text = string.Format("Unhandled Exception{1}Exception Message: {0}{1}Excetion Source: {2}{1}StackTrace: {3}", e.Message, Environment.NewLine, e.Source, e.StackTrace);
			SystemEnvironment.Logger.Log(text, (Category)1, (Priority)1);
			ILoggerFacade logger = SystemEnvironment.Logger;
			DebugLogger val = (DebugLogger)(object)((logger is DebugLogger) ? logger : null);
			if (bDisplayMessageOnException && val != null && val.FilePath != null)
			{
				MMMessageBox.Show(string.Format(ApwnResources.PlotUnhandledException, val.FilePath), ApwnResources.AspenPlus, (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)16, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
			}
		}

		public void UpdateWizardData()
		{
			List<string> list = new List<string>(CurveList.GetSelectedCurves());
			int count = list.Count;
			_selectedCurves.Clear();
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				_selectedCurves.Add(list[i]);
			}
		}

		private string GetTimeConfig()
		{
			string varName = "DISTRECV(#)_MC(?)";
			string varName2 = "DISTRECV(#)_MMC(?)";
			string text = "Moles";
			string text2 = "Mass";
			string text3 = "DISTRECV(#)_M";
			string text4 = "DISTRECV(#)_MM";
			string text5 = "";
			string text6 = "";
			string text7 = "Total Moles";
			string text8 = "Total Mass";
			string text9 = "";
			for (int i = 0; i < _basisRadBtnList.Count; i++)
			{
				if (_basisRadBtnList[i].IsChecked == true)
				{
					_sTitleID = _strTBasis[i];
					break;
				}
			}
			if (_sTitleID == WpfPLBatchSepResources.Mole)
			{
				text9 = text;
				_VarName = varName;
				text5 = text3;
				text6 = text7;
			}
			if (_sTitleID == WpfPLBatchSepResources.Mass)
			{
				text9 = text2;
				_VarName = varName2;
				text5 = text4;
				text6 = text8;
			}
			string empty = string.Empty;
			string text10 = string.Empty;
			string text11 = string.Empty;
			_sTimeConfig = string.Empty;
			_sYAxisName = string.Empty;
			int count = _selectedCurves.Count;
			int nStage = GetNStage();
			string blockID = GetBlockID();
			string text12 = "";
			int num = 0;
			if (count > 0 && nStage > 0 && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(blockID))
			{
				_sTimeConfig = _sTimePlot;
				for (int j = 0; j < count; j++)
				{
					string text13 = _selectedCurves[j];
					string text14;
					if (text13 == _sTotal)
					{
						text14 = text5;
						text14 = text14.Replace("#", _sSelectedItem);
						text10 = text10 + "$" + text14;
						text11 = text11 + "$" + text6;
						continue;
					}
					text14 = _VarName;
					text14 = text14.Replace("#", _sSelectedItem);
					text14 = text14.Replace("?", text13);
					text10 = text10 + "$" + text14;
					_sTimeConfig = _sTimeConfig + "!" + text14;
					text11 = text11 + "$" + text9 + "(" + text13 + ")";
				}
				_sTitle = WpfPLBatchSepResources.DistillateReceiverHoldup_Title;
				_sTitle = _sTitle.Replace("%ID%", blockID);
				_sTitle = _sTitle.Replace("#", _sSelectedItem);
				_sTitle = _sTitle.Replace("%TID%", _sTitleID);
				text12 = _sTitle;
				num = text12.IndexOf(':');
				text12 = text12.Substring(num + 1);
				empty = empty + "Type:$" + _sTimePlot;
				empty = empty + "!VarPaths:" + text10;
				empty = empty + "!nStage:$" + nStage;
				empty = empty + "!PlotTitle:$" + text12;
				empty = empty + "!YAxisNames:" + text11;
				empty = empty + "!BlockPath:$" + blockID;
				empty = empty + "!TabTitle:$" + _sTitle;
				empty += "!PlotSettings:$<Scpc ShowMultipleYAxes=\"false\">\r\n\t                    <OtherSettings>\r\n\t\t                    <ShowTitle>true</ShowTitle>\r\n\t\t                    <ShowLegend>true</ShowLegend>\r\n\t                    </OtherSettings>\r\n                    </Scpc>";
				_sTimeConfig = empty;
			}
			return _sTimeConfig;
		}

		private void ExecuteOKCommand(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			UpdateWizardData();
			_currentConfigValue = GetTimeConfig();
			_help.CloseForm((UserControl)this, (bool?)true);
		}

		private void CanExecuteOKCommand(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			int num = CurveList.GetSelectedCurves().Count();
			if (num == CurveList.Curves.Count)
			{
				CurveList.IsCheckAll = true;
			}
			else
			{
				string[] selectedCurves = CurveList.GetSelectedCurves();
				CurveList.IsCheckAll = false;
				CurveList.SetSelectedCurves(selectedCurves);
			}
			e.CanExecute = num != 0;
		}

		private void ExecuteCancelCommand(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			_currentConfigValue = string.Empty;
			_sYAxisName = string.Empty;
			_sTitle = string.Empty;
			_help.CloseForm((UserControl)this, (bool?)false);
		}

		private void SetVariables()
		{
			_strTBasis = new string[2]
			{
				WpfPLBatchSepResources.Mole,
				WpfPLBatchSepResources.Mass
			};
		}

		private void SetControls()
		{
			_basisRadBtnList = new List<RadioButton>();
			_basisRadBtnList.Add(radBtnMole);
			_basisRadBtnList.Add(radBtnMass);
			_basisRadBtnList[0].IsChecked = true;
		}

		private void SetComboBox()
		{
			_selComboIndex = 0;
			int num = 1;
			object nodeCxs = _nodeCxs;
			CxsNode val = (CxsNode)((nodeCxs is CxsNode) ? nodeCxs : null);
			if (val != null)
			{
				CxsCase val2 = val.Case;
				string text = val.Path + ".Input.NDISTRECV";
				if (val2.BatchGet(text, "value") != null)
				{
					num = (int)val2.BatchGet(text, "value");
				}
			}
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			for (int i = 1; i <= num; i++)
			{
				list.Add(i.ToString());
			}
			distReceiver.ItemsSource = list;
			distReceiver.SelectedIndex = _selComboIndex;
		}

		private void distReceiver_SelectionChanged(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			ComboBox comboBox = sender as ComboBox;
			_sSelectedItem = comboBox.SelectedItem as string;
			int result = 0;
			if (int.TryParse(_sSelectedItem, out result))
			{
				_selComboIndex = result - 1;
			}
			else
			{
				_selComboIndex = 0;
			}
		}

		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "4.0.0.0")]
		public void InitializeComponent()
		{
			if (!_contentLoaded)
			{
				_contentLoaded = true;
				Uri resourceLocator = new Uri("/WpfPLBatchSep;component/receiverholdup.xaml", UriKind.Relative);
				Application.LoadComponent(this, resourceLocator);
			}
		}

		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "4.0.0.0")]
		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		void IComponentConnector.Connect(int connectionId, object target)
		{
			switch (connectionId)
			{
			case 1:
				distReceiver = (ComboBox)target;
				distReceiver.SelectionChanged += distReceiver_SelectionChanged;
				break;
			case 2:
				radBtnMole = (RadioButton)target;
				break;
			case 3:
				radBtnMass = (RadioButton)target;
				break;
			case 4:
				CurveList = (CurvesSelectorControl)target;
				break;
			default:
				_contentLoaded = true;
				break;
			}
		}
	}
	internal static class StringHelper
	{
		internal static string GetStringBetweenParentheses(this string text)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(text))
			{
				return null;
			}
			int num = text.IndexOf("(") + 1;
			int num2 = text.IndexOf(")", num);
			if (num < 0 || num2 < 0)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return text.Substring(num, num2 - num);
		}
	}
	internal static class LocalizedVariablesTagsHelper
	{
		internal static string AsTag(this string value)
		{
			return "(" + value + ")";
		}

		internal static bool ContainsTag(this IEnumerable<string> list, string tag, bool itemsHaveTagFormat = false)
		{
			return list.AsParallel().Any((string item) => string.Equals(itemsHaveTagFormat ? item : item.AsTag(), tag));
		}
	}

		List<string> IRibbonPlotWizardType.FieldDataOptionList(string sFieldName)
		{
			if (sFieldName.CompareTo(_tempUnitFieldId) == 0)
			{
				List<string> list = new List<string>();
				if (_mvarControlContext != null)
				{
					dynamic val = _mvarControlContext.Pot.T.ValidValues("units");
					{
						foreach (object item2 in val)
						{
							string item = (string)(dynamic)item2;
							list.Add(item);
						}
						return list;
					}
				}
				return null;
			}
			if (sFieldName.CompareTo(_stageOrderFieldId) == 0)
			{
				return new List<string> { _ascending, _descending };
			}
			if (sFieldName.CompareTo(_stageOnFieldId) == 0)
			{
				return new List<string> { _xAxis, _yAxis };
			}
			return null;
		}

		void IRibbonPlotWizardType.GetFieldData(string sFieldName, ref string sCurrentVal)

					sCurrentVal = _mvarControlContext.Pot.T.Units("CurrentUnits");
				}
				else

				}
			}
		}

		private int GetNStage()
		{

			if (val != null)
			{

				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		private string GetBlockID()
		{
			string result = "";
			object nodeCxs = _nodeCxs;
			CxsNode val = (CxsNode)((nodeCxs is CxsNode) ? nodeCxs : null);
			if (val != null)
			{

				string path = val.Path;

				try

					foreach (string item in new List<string> { "Top.appModelV8.", "Blocks.", "Data.", "Controllers." })

		}

		RibbonControlType IRibbonPlotWizardType.GetFieldCtrlType(string sField)
		{
			if (sField.CompareTo(_tempUnitFieldId) != 0 && sField.CompareTo(_stageOrderFieldId) != 0 && sField.CompareTo(_stageOnFieldId) != 0)
			{
				return (RibbonControlType)0;
			}
			return (RibbonControlType)1;
		}

		void IRibbonPlotWizardType.GetFieldStyle(string sFieldName, ref string sCurrentVal)
		{
			if (sFieldName.CompareTo(_stageOrderFieldId) == 0)
			{
				if (_wizardPlot.PlotVal.ReverseXAxis)
				{
					sCurrentVal = _descending;
				}
				else
				{
					sCurrentVal = _ascending;
				}
			}
			else if (sFieldName.CompareTo(_stageOnFieldId) == 0)
			{
				if (_wizardPlot.PlotVal.SwapAxises)
				{
					sCurrentVal = _yAxis;
				}
				else
				{
					sCurrentVal = _xAxis;
				}
			}
		}

		void IRibbonPlotWizardType.SetFieldStyle(string sFieldName, string sValue)
		{
			if (sFieldName.CompareTo(_stageOrderFieldId) == 0)
			{
				if (sValue.CompareTo(_ascending) == 0)
				{
					_wizardPlot.PlotVal.ReverseXAxis = false;
				}
				else
				{
					_wizardPlot.PlotVal.ReverseXAxis = true;
				}
			}
			else if (sFieldName.CompareTo(_stageOnFieldId) == 0)
			{
				if (sValue.CompareTo(_yAxis) == 0)
				{
					_wizardPlot.PlotVal.SwapAxises = true;
				}
				else
				{
					_wizardPlot.PlotVal.SwapAxises = false;
				}
			}
		}

		private void GetConfigString()
		{
			int nStage = GetNStage();
			string blockID = GetBlockID();
			_currentConfigValue = string.Empty;
			_sYAxisName = WpfPLBatchSepResources.Temperature;
			_sTitle = WpfPLBatchSepResources.BlockTitle + WpfPLBatchSepResources.TemperatureProfile;
			_sTitle = _sTitle.Replace("%ID%", blockID);
			string sTitle = _sTitle;
			int num = sTitle.IndexOf(':');
			sTitle = sTitle.Substring(num + 1);
			string text = "";
			text = text + "Type:$" + sProfilePlot;
			text += "!VarPaths:$T(*)";
			text = text + "!nStage:$" + nStage;
			text = text + "!PlotTitle:$" + sTitle;
			text = text + "!YAxisNames:$" + _sYAxisName;
			text = text + "!BlockPath:$" + blockID;
			text = text + "!TabTitle:$" + _sTitle;
			text += "!PlotSettings:$<Scpc ShowMultipleYAxes=\"true\">\r\n\t                    <OtherSettings>\r\n\t\t                    <ShowTitle>true</ShowTitle>\r\n\t\t                    <ShowLegend>false</ShowLegend>\r\n\t                    </OtherSettings>\r\n                    </Scpc>";
			_currentConfigValue = text;
		}

		void IRibbonPlotWizardType.GetConfigData(ref string sConfigValue)

			sConfigValue = _currentConfigValue;
		}

		void IRibbonPlotWizardType.Context(object HNode, ref object HWizardPlot, ref int ierror)
		{
			if (HWizardPlot == null || HNode == null)
			{
				ierror = 1;
				return;
			}
			_context = HNode as IHNode;
			_wizardPlot = HWizardPlot as IHWizardPlot;
			bool flag = false;
			_bHasInputDialog = false;
			flag = true;
			if (_mvarControlContext != null)
			{
				_ = (string)_mvarControlContext.Pot.T.Units("CurrentUnits");
			}
			if (!flag)
			{
				ierror = 1;
			}
			else
			{
				_wizardPlot.RemoveAllDependentVariable();
			}
		}

		public void UpdateWizardData()
		{
			int nStage = GetNStage();
			double[] array = new double[nStage];
			for (int i = 0; i < nStage; i++)
			{
				array[i] = 0.0;
			}
			if (_currentTempUnit == "" && _mvarControlContext != null)
			{
				_currentTempUnit = _mvarControlContext.Pot.T.Units("CurrentUnits");
			}
			if (_mvarControlContext != null)
			{
				array[0] = _mvarControlContext.Condenser.T.value(_currentTempUnit);
				for (int j = 2; j < nStage; j++)
				{
					array[j - 1] = _mvarControlContext.stage(j).T.value(_currentTempUnit);
				}
				array[nStage - 1] = _mvarControlContext.Pot.T.value(_currentTempUnit);
			}
			_wizardPlot.RemoveAllDependentVariable();
			_wizardPlot.PlotVal.NVariables = 1;
			_wizardPlot.PlotVal.NXVariables = 1;
			_wizardPlot.PlotVal.NPoints = nStage;
			string text = "Temperature  " + _currentTempUnit;
			((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_Label(0, text);
			((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_YAxisTitle(Type.Missing, text);
			for (int k = 0; k < nStage; k++)
			{
				double num = k + 1;
				((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_XData(0, k, (short)0, (object)num);
				((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_YData(0, k, (short)0, (object)array[k]);
			}
			_help.SaveUnitsComboText(_context.Application.Tree, _wizardPlot, _currentTempUnit);
		}

		private void SetVariables()
		{
			_VarNames = new string[1] { "Pot.T" };
		}

		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "4.0.0.0")]
		public void InitializeComponent()
		{
			if (!_contentLoaded)
			{
				_contentLoaded = true;
				Uri resourceLocator = new Uri("/WpfPLBatchSep;component/temperature.xaml", UriKind.Relative);
				Application.LoadComponent(this, resourceLocator);
			}
		}

		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "4.0.0.0")]
		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		void IComponentConnector.Connect(int connectionId, object target)
		{
			_contentLoaded = true;
		}

		List<string> IRibbonPlotWizardType.FieldDataOptionList(string sFieldName)
		{
			if (sFieldName.CompareTo(_tempUnitFieldId) == 0)
			{
				List<string> list = new List<string>();
				_help.LoadUnitsList(_context.Application.Tree, _wizardPlot, list);
				return list;
			}
			if (sFieldName.CompareTo(_timeUnitFieldId) == 0)
			{
				List<string> list2 = new List<string>();
				_help.LoadXUnitsList(_context.Application.Tree, _wizardPlot, list2);
				return list2;
			}
			if (sFieldName.CompareTo(_timeOrderFieldId) == 0)
			{
				return new List<string> { _ascending, _descending };
			}
			if (sFieldName.CompareTo(_timeOnFieldId) == 0)
			{
				return new List<string> { _xAxis, _yAxis };
			}
			return null;
		}

		void IRibbonPlotWizardType.GetFieldData(string sFieldName, ref string sCurrentVal)
		{
			if (sFieldName.CompareTo(_tempUnitFieldId) == 0)
			{
				sCurrentVal = _help.GetUnitString(_context.Application.Tree, _wizardPlot);
			}
			else if (sFieldName.CompareTo(_timeUnitFieldId) == 0)
			{
				sCurrentVal = _help.GetXUnitString(_context.Application.Tree, _wizardPlot);
			}
		}

		void IRibbonPlotWizardType.SetFieldData(string sFieldName, string sValue)
		{
			if (sFieldName.CompareTo(_tempUnitFieldId) == 0)
			{
				_currentTempUnit = sValue;
			}
			else if (sFieldName.CompareTo(_timeUnitFieldId) == 0)
			{
				_currentTimeUnit = sValue;
			}
		}

		void IRibbonPlotWizardType.ShowInputDialog()
		{
		}

		RibbonControlType IRibbonPlotWizardType.GetFieldCtrlType(string sField)
		{
			if (sField.CompareTo(_tempUnitFieldId) != 0 && sField.CompareTo(_timeUnitFieldId) != 0 && sField.CompareTo(_timeOnFieldId) != 0 && sField.CompareTo(_timeOrderFieldId) != 0)
			{
				return (RibbonControlType)0;
			}
			return (RibbonControlType)1;
		}

		void IRibbonPlotWizardType.GetFieldStyle(string sFieldName, ref string sCurrentVal)
		{
			if (sFieldName.CompareTo(_timeOrderFieldId) == 0)
			{
				if (_wizardPlot.PlotVal.ReverseXAxis)
				{
					sCurrentVal = _descending;
				}
				else
				{
					sCurrentVal = _ascending;
				}
			}
			else if (sFieldName.CompareTo(_timeOnFieldId) == 0)
			{
				if (_wizardPlot.PlotVal.SwapAxises)
				{
					sCurrentVal = _yAxis;
				}
				else
				{
					sCurrentVal = _xAxis;
				}
			}
		}

		void IRibbonPlotWizardType.SetFieldStyle(string sFieldName, string sValue)
		{
			if (sFieldName.CompareTo(_timeOrderFieldId) == 0)
			{
				if (sValue.CompareTo(_ascending) == 0)
				{
					_wizardPlot.PlotVal.ReverseXAxis = false;
				}
				else
				{
					_wizardPlot.PlotVal.ReverseXAxis = true;
				}
			}
			else if (sFieldName.CompareTo(_timeOnFieldId) == 0)
			{
				if (sValue.CompareTo(_yAxis) == 0)
				{
					_wizardPlot.PlotVal.SwapAxises = true;
				}
				else
				{
					_wizardPlot.PlotVal.SwapAxises = false;
				}
			}
		}

		void IRibbonPlotWizardType.GetConfigData(ref string sConfigValue)
		{
			sConfigValue = _currentConfigValue;
		}

		void IRibbonPlotWizardType.SetConfigData(string sValue)
		{
			_currentConfigValue = sValue;
		}

		void IRibbonPlotWizardType.Context(object HNode, ref object HWizardPlot, ref int ierror)
		{
			if (HWizardPlot == null || HNode == null)
			{
				ierror = 1;
				return;
			}
			_context = HNode as IHNode;
			_wizardPlot = HWizardPlot as IHWizardPlot;
			_mvarControlContext = _context.FindNode("Data").get_AttributeValue((short)0, Type.Missing);
			_host = null;
			_host = _mvarControlContext.ExportHost;
			if (_host != null)
			{
				_varProfService = _host.GetService("ASCVariableProfile");
			}
			bool flag = false;
			if (_mvarControlContext.DynConverged.value)
			{
				flag = true;
			}
			if (!flag)
			{
				ierror = 1;
				return;
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_currentConfigValue))
			{
				RestoreConfigValue(_currentConfigValue);
			}
			_wizardPlot.RemoveAllDependentVariable();
		}

		public void UpdateWizardData()
		{
			_wizardPlot.RemoveAllDependentVariable();
			if (_currentTempUnit == "")
			{
				_currentTempUnit = _mvarControlContext.Pot.T.Units("CurrentUnits");
			}
			int num = 10;
			_ = new double[num];
			double num2 = _mvarControlContext.CurrentTime.value;
			double num3 = num2 / (double)num;
			string obj = _mvarControlContext.BlockID.value;
			int num4 = 0;
			_wizardPlot.RemoveAllDependentVariable();
			_wizardPlot.PlotVal.NVariables = 3;
			_wizardPlot.PlotVal.NXVariables = 1;
			_wizardPlot.PlotVal.NPoints = num;
			string text = obj + ".T(2)";
			dynamic profileByName = _varProfService.GetProfileByName(text, 0.0, num2, num3);
			int num5 = 0;
			foreach (double item in profileByName)
			{
				double num7 = (double)num5 * num3;
				((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_XData(0, num5, (short)0, (object)num7);
				((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_YData(num4, num5, (short)0, (object)item);
				num5++;
			}
			((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_Label(num4, "Temperature");
			num4++;
			_help.SaveUnitsComboText(_context.Application.Tree, _wizardPlot, _currentTempUnit);
			_help.SaveXUnitsComboText(_context.Application.Tree, _wizardPlot, _currentTimeUnit);
			_currentConfigValue = SaveConfigValue();
		}

		private void UpdateLocVar(dynamic ACMProf, double[] val)
		{
			int num = 0;
			foreach (object item in ACMProf)
			{
				double num2 = (double)(dynamic)item;
				val[num] = num2;
				num++;
			}
		}

		private void ExecuteOKCommand(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			UpdateWizardData();
			_help.CloseForm((UserControl)this, (bool?)true);
		}

		private void CanExecuteOKCommand(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			int num = 0;
			for (int i = 0; i < _chkArr.Count(); i++)
			{
				if (_chkArr[i].IsEnabled && _chkArr[i].IsChecked.Value)
				{
					num++;
					break;
				}
			}
			e.CanExecute = num != 0;
		}

		private void ExecuteCancelCommand(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			_help.CloseForm((UserControl)this, (bool?)false);
		}

		private void SetVariables()
		{
			_VarNames = new string[3] { "Pot.T", "Condenser.T", "Drum(1).T" };
		}

		private void SetControls()
		{
		}

		private string SaveConfigValue()
		{
			_ = string.Empty;
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			int num = 0;
			stringBuilder.Append("temperature=");
			for (int i = 0; i < _chkArr.Count(); i++)
			{
				if (_chkArr[i].IsChecked == true)
				{
					if (num > 0)
					{
						stringBuilder.Append(",");
					}
					stringBuilder.Append(i.ToString());
					num++;
				}
			}
			stringBuilder.Append(";");
			num = 0;
			stringBuilder.Append("tempEnable=");
			for (int j = 0; j < _chkArr.Count(); j++)
			{
				if (_chkArr[j].IsEnabled)
				{
					if (num > 0)
					{
						stringBuilder.Append(",");
					}
					stringBuilder.Append(j.ToString());
					num++;
				}
			}
			stringBuilder.Append(";");
			return stringBuilder.ToString();
		}

		private void RestoreConfigValue(string ConfigString)
		{
			string[] array = ConfigString.Split(';');
			int num = 0;
			string[] array2 = array[num].Substring(array[num].IndexOf('=') + 1).Split(',');
			foreach (string value in array2)
			{
				_chkArr[Convert.ToInt16(value)].IsChecked = true;
			}
			num++;
			array2 = array[num].Substring(array[num].IndexOf('=') + 1).Split(',');
			foreach (string value2 in array2)
			{
				_chkArr[Convert.ToInt16(value2)].IsEnabled = true;
			}
		}

		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "4.0.0.0")]
		public void InitializeComponent()
		{
			if (!_contentLoaded)
			{
				_contentLoaded = true;
				Uri resourceLocator = new Uri("/WpfPLBatchSep;component/temperaturetime.xaml", UriKind.Relative);
				Application.LoadComponent(this, resourceLocator);
			}
		}

		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "4.0.0.0")]
		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		void IComponentConnector.Connect(int connectionId, object target)
		{
			_contentLoaded = true;
		}
	}
	public class Variablelist
	{
		public List<string> childnode = new List<string>();

		public List<string> label_selectedRow = new List<string>();

		public string VariablesName { get; set; }

		public string child_Variables { get; set; }

		public Variablelist()
		{
		}

		public Variablelist(string vnames, string sub_vnames, Variablelist parentNode = null)
		{
			VariablesName = vnames;
			child_Variables = sub_vnames;
		}

		public void AddchildToVariable(Variablelist vobject, List<string> childnode, TreeViewItem selectednode)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < childnode.Count; i++)
			{
				vobject.childnode.Add(childnode[i]);
				vobject.label_selectedRow.Add(selectednode.Header?.ToString() + "." + vobject.VariablesName + "." + vobject.childnode[i]);
			}
		}
	}

		public bool IsStagesSectionEnabled

			{
				_isStagesSectionEnabled = value;
				if (!_isStagesSectionEnabled && CB_Stages.HasItems)
				{
					CB_Stages.Items.Clear();
				}
				IsAllStagesChecked = _isStagesSectionEnabled && IsAllStagesChecked;
				OnPropertyChanged("IsStagesSectionEnabled");
			}
		}

		public bool IsAllStagesChecked
		{
			get
			{
				return _isAllStagesChecked;
			}
			set
			{
				_isAllStagesChecked = value;
				OnPropertyChanged("IsAllStagesChecked");
				OnPropertyChanged("IsCBStagesEnabled");
			}
		}

		public bool IsCBStagesEnabled
		{
			get
			{
				if (IsStagesSectionEnabled)
				{
					return !IsAllStagesChecked;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public Custom()
		{
			//IL_0009: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0013: Expected O, but got Unknown
			InitializeComponent();
			base.DataContext = this;
			SetVariables();
			SetControls();
			_bHasInputDialog = true;
			if (_imgBitmap == null)
			{
				Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly();
				Stream manifestResourceStream = Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly().GetManifestResourceStream("WpfPLBatchSep.Images.Custom1Plot.ico");
				_imgBitmap = System.Drawing.Image.FromStream(manifestResourceStream);
			}
			CommandBinding commandBinding = new CommandBinding(OKCommand, ExecuteOKCommand, CanExecuteOKCommand);
			CommandBinding commandBinding2 = new CommandBinding(CancelCommand, ExecuteCancelCommand);
			base.CommandBindings.Add(commandBinding);
			base.CommandBindings.Add(commandBinding2);
			CurveList.Header = WpfPLBatchSepResources.Select_Comp;
		}

		List<string> IRibbonPlotWizardType.FieldDataOptionList(string sFieldName)
		{
			if (sFieldName.CompareTo(_stageOrderFieldId) == 0)
			{
				return new List<string> { _ascending, _descending };
			}
			if (sFieldName.CompareTo(_stageOnFieldId) == 0)
			{
				return new List<string> { _xAxis, _yAxis };
			}
			return null;
		}

		}

		private void SetFaseRadBtns(string ValidPhases)
		{
			switch (ValidPhases)
			{
			case "Vapor-Liquid":
			case "Vapor-Liquid-LiquidCondenser":
			case "Vapor-Liquid-FreeWaterCondenser":
				_phaseRadBtnList[0].IsEnabled = true;
				_phaseRadBtnList[1].IsEnabled = true;
				break;
			case "Vapor-Liquid-Liquid":
				_phaseRadBtnList[0].IsEnabled = true;
				_phaseRadBtnList[1].IsEnabled = true;
				_phaseRadBtnList[2].IsEnabled = true;
				_phaseRadBtnList[3].IsEnabled = true;
				break;
			case "Vapor-Liquid-FreeWater":
				_phaseRadBtnList[0].IsEnabled = true;
				_phaseRadBtnList[1].IsEnabled = true;
				_phaseRadBtnList[2].IsEnabled = true;
				break;
			default:
				_phaseRadBtnList[0].IsEnabled = true;
				_phaseRadBtnList[1].IsEnabled = true;
				break;
			}
		}

		private int GetNStage()
		{
			int result = 0;
			object nodeCxs = _nodeCxs;
			CxsNode val = (CxsNode)((nodeCxs is CxsNode) ? nodeCxs : null);
			if (val != null)
			{
				CxsCase val2 = val.Case;
				string text = val.Path + ".Input.NSTAGE";
				if (val2.BatchGet(text, "value") != null)
				{
					result = (int)val2.BatchGet(text, "value");
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		private string GetBlockID()
		{
			string result = "";
			object nodeCxs = _nodeCxs;
			CxsNode val = (CxsNode)((nodeCxs is CxsNode) ? nodeCxs : null);
			if (val != null)
			{
				_ = val.Case;
				string path = val.Path;
				path = path.TrimEnd('.');
				try
				{
					foreach (string item in new List<string> { "Top.appModelV8.", "Blocks.", "Data.", "Controllers." })
					{
						path = path.Replace(item, "");
					}
					result = path;
				}
				catch
				{
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		private void InitDialogSettings()
		{
			PotTVI.IsSelected = true;
			if (PotTVI.IsSelected)
			{
				LoadVariablestoGridControl();
			}
			if (SelectedVariablesDataGrid.ItemsSource != null)
			{
				if (obCollection_SelVars.Count > 0)
				{
					obCollection_SelVars.Clear();
				}
				SelectedVariablesDataGrid.ItemsSource = null;
			}
			if (DoneButton.IsEnabled)
			{
				DoneButton.IsEnabled = false;
			}
			object nodeCxs = _nodeCxs;
			CxsNode val = (CxsNode)((nodeCxs is CxsNode) ? nodeCxs : null);
			if (val == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			CxsCase val2 = val.Case;
			string text = val.Path + ".Input.VALIDPHASES";
			if (val2.BatchGet(text, "value") != null)
			{
				string key = (string)val2.BatchGet(text, "value");
				string faseRadBtns = "";
				if (sDicValidFases.ContainsKey(key))
				{
					faseRadBtns = sDicValidFases[key];
				}
				SetFaseRadBtns(faseRadBtns);
			}
		}

			}
		}

		void IRibbonPlotWizardType.ShowInputDialog()
		{
			//IL_001d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0023: Expected O, but got Unknown
			InitCurveList();
			InitDialogSettings();
			getInputsettingValue();
			_currentConfigValue = string.Empty;
			PlotWizardTemplate val = new PlotWizardTemplate();
			((FrameworkElement)(object)val).MinWidth = 400.0;
			((ContentControl)(object)val).Content = this;
			MMHelp.SetHelpFile((DependencyObject)(object)val, _help.GetHelpFileName());
			MMHelp.SetHelpId((DependencyObject)(object)val, (int?)(int)_help.GetHelpID());
			((FrameworkElement)(object)val).Cursor = Cursors.Arrow;
			try
			{
				val._ShowInTaskbar = true;
				val._SizeToContent = SizeToContent.Manual;
				((Window)(object)val).ShowDialog();
			}
			catch (Exception e)
			{
				LogAndDisplayErrorMessage(e, bDisplayMessageOnException: true);
				((Window)(object)val).Close();
			}
			((ContentControl)(object)val).Content = null;
		}

		private void getInputsettingValue()
		{
			object nodeCxs = _nodeCxs;
			CxsNode val = (CxsNode)((nodeCxs is CxsNode) ? nodeCxs : null);
			string text = null;
			string text2 = null;
			string text3 = null;
			int num = 0;
			int num2 = 0;
			int num3 = 0;
			int num4 = 0;
			int num5 = 0;
			int num6 = 0;
			if (val == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			CxsCase val2 = val.Case;
			string path = val.Path;
			string text4 = path + ".Input.CONFIG";
			if (val2.BatchGet(text4, "value") != null)
			{
				str_inputconfigValue = (string)val2.BatchGet(text4, "value");
			}
			str_inputconfigValue = str_inputconfigValue.Trim();
			str_inputconfigValue = str_inputconfigValue.ToUpper();
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text4) && str_inputconfigValue == "POT")
			{
				for (int i = 1; i < CategoriesTreeView.Items.Count; i++)
				{
					CondenserTVI.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
					StageTVI.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
					DistillateReceiversTVI.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
					RefluxDrumTVI.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
					RefluxSplitterTVI.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				}
				string text5 = path + ".Input.H_TRANS_MODE";
				if (val2.BatchGet(text5, "value") != null)
				{
					text3 = (string)val2.BatchGet(text5, "value");
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text5) && text3 == "SHORTCUT")
				{
					JacketHeatingTVI.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
					JacketCoolingTVI.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
					CoilsHeatingTVI.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
					CoilsCoolingTVI.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
					ExchangerHeatingTVI.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
					ExchangerCoolingTVI.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				}
				else
				{
					string text6 = path + ".Input.JA_HEATING";
					if (val2.BatchGet(text6, "value") != null)
					{
						num = (int)val2.BatchGet(text6, "value");
					}
					if (num == 1)
					{
						JacketHeatingTVI.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
					}
					else
					{
						JacketHeatingTVI.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
					}
					string text7 = path + ".Input.JA_COOLING";
					if (val2.BatchGet(text7, "value") != null)
					{
						num2 = (int)val2.BatchGet(text7, "value");
					}
					if (num2 == 1)
					{
						JacketCoolingTVI.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
					}
					else
					{
						JacketCoolingTVI.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
					}
					string text8 = path + ".Input.COILSHEATING";
					if (val2.BatchGet(text8, "value") != null)
					{
						num3 = (int)val2.BatchGet(text8, "value");
					}
					if (num3 == 1)
					{
						CoilsHeatingTVI.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
					}
					else
					{
						CoilsHeatingTVI.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
					}
					string text9 = path + ".Input.COILSCOOLING";
					if (val2.BatchGet(text9, "value") != null)
					{
						num4 = (int)val2.BatchGet(text9, "value");
					}
					if (num4 == 1)
					{
						CoilsCoolingTVI.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
					}
					else
					{
						CoilsCoolingTVI.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
					}
					string text10 = path + ".Input.EXTHXHEATING";
					if (val2.BatchGet(text10, "value") != null)
					{
						num5 = (int)val2.BatchGet(text10, "value");
					}
					if (num5 == 1)
					{
						ExchangerHeatingTVI.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
					}
					else
					{
						ExchangerHeatingTVI.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
					}
					string text11 = path + ".Input.EXTHXCOOLING";
					if (val2.BatchGet(text11, "value") != null)
					{
						num6 = (int)val2.BatchGet(text11, "value");
					}
					if (num6 == 1)
					{
						ExchangerCoolingTVI.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
					}
					else
					{
						ExchangerCoolingTVI.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
					}
				}
				string text12 = path + ".Input.SDRECV_STRM";
				object obj = val2.BatchGet(text12, "has_children");
				if ("1" == obj.ToString())
				{
					SideDrawReceiverTVI.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
				}
				else
				{
					SideDrawReceiverTVI.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text4) || !(str_inputconfigValue == "COLUMN"))
				{
					return;
				}
				string text13 = path + ".Input.CONDENSER";
				if (val2.BatchGet(text13, "value") != null)
				{
					text = (string)val2.BatchGet(text13, "value");
				}
				if (text == "PARTIAL2")
				{
					SecondCondenserTVI.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
				}
				else
				{
					SecondCondenserTVI.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				}
				string text14 = path + ".Input.REFLUXDRUM";
				if (val2.BatchGet(text14, "value") != null)
				{
					text2 = (string)val2.BatchGet(text14, "value");
				}
				if (text2 == "YES")
				{
					RefluxDrumTVI.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
				}
				else
				{
					RefluxDrumTVI.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				}
				string text15 = path + ".Input.H_TRANS_MODE";
				if (val2.BatchGet(text15, "value") != null)
				{
					text3 = (string)val2.BatchGet(text15, "value");
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text15) && text3 == "SHORTCUT")
				{
					JacketHeatingTVI.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
					JacketCoolingTVI.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
					CoilsHeatingTVI.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
					CoilsCoolingTVI.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
					ExchangerHeatingTVI.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
					ExchangerCoolingTVI.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				}
				else
				{
					string text16 = path + ".Input.JA_HEATING";
					if (val2.BatchGet(text16, "value") != null)
					{
						num = (int)val2.BatchGet(text16, "value");
					}
					if (num == 1)
					{
						JacketHeatingTVI.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
					}
					else
					{
						JacketHeatingTVI.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
					}
					string text17 = path + ".Input.JA_COOLING";
					if (val2.BatchGet(text17, "value") != null)
					{
						num2 = (int)val2.BatchGet(text17, "value");
					}
					if (num2 == 1)
					{
						JacketCoolingTVI.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
					}
					else
					{
						JacketCoolingTVI.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
					}
					string text18 = path + ".Input.COILSHEATING";
					if (val2.BatchGet(text18, "value") != null)
					{
						num3 = (int)val2.BatchGet(text18, "value");
					}
					if (num3 == 1)
					{
						CoilsHeatingTVI.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
					}
					else
					{
						CoilsHeatingTVI.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
					}
					string text19 = path + ".Input.COILSCOOLING";
					if (val2.BatchGet(text19, "value") != null)
					{
						num4 = (int)val2.BatchGet(text19, "value");
					}
					if (num4 == 1)
					{
						CoilsCoolingTVI.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
					}
					else

					}

					if (val2.BatchGet(text20, "value") != null)

					}
					else

		private void getJacketHeatingOption(DataTable allVariables_table)
		{
			object nodeCxs = _nodeCxs;
			CxsNode val = (CxsNode)((nodeCxs is CxsNode) ? nodeCxs : null);
			string text = null;
			if (val == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			CxsCase val2 = val.Case;
			string text2 = val.Path + ".Input.JH_OPT";
			if (val2.BatchGet(text2, "value") != null)
			{
				text = (string)val2.BatchGet(text2, "value");
				string selectedOption = "";
				if (strDic_JktHeating_options.ContainsKey(text))
				{
					selectedOption = strDic_JktHeating_options[text];
				}
				_rowVisibilitySetter.SetForJacketHeatingOptions(selectedOption, allVariables_table, val);
			}
		}

		private void getJacketCoolingOption(DataTable allVariables_table)
		{
			object nodeCxs = _nodeCxs;
			CxsNode val = (CxsNode)((nodeCxs is CxsNode) ? nodeCxs : null);
			string text = null;
			if (val == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			CxsCase val2 = val.Case;
			string text2 = val.Path + ".Input.JC_OPT";
			if (val2.BatchGet(text2, "value") != null)
			{
				text = (string)val2.BatchGet(text2, "value");
				string selectedOption = "";
				if (strDic_JktCooling_options.ContainsKey(text))
				{
					selectedOption = strDic_JktCooling_options[text];
				}
				_rowVisibilitySetter.SetForJachekCoolingOptions(selectedOption, allVariables_table);
			}
		}

		private void InitCurveList()
		{
			object nodeCxs = _nodeCxs;
			CxsNode val = (CxsNode)((nodeCxs is CxsNode) ? nodeCxs : null);
			if (val == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			CxsCase val2 = val.Case;
			string path = val.Path;
			int length = path.IndexOf("Blocks", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
			string text = path.Substring(0, length);
			text += "Components.Specifications.Input.TYPE";
			object obj = val2.BatchGet(text, "child_ids");
			if (obj != null)
			{
				List<string> list = (from x in obj.ToString().Split()
					where !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(x)
					select x).ToList();
				if (list.Count > 0)
				{
					CurveList.IsCheckAll = false;
					CurveList.SetCurves(list.ToArray());
				}
			}
		}

		private void LogAndDisplayErrorMessage(Exception e, bool bDisplayMessageOnException)
		{
			//IL_007a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			string text = string.Format("Unhandled Exception{1}Exception Message: {0}{1}Excetion Source: {2}{1}StackTrace: {3}", e.Message, Environment.NewLine, e.Source, e.StackTrace);
			SystemEnvironment.Logger.Log(text, (Category)1, (Priority)1);
			ILoggerFacade logger = SystemEnvironment.Logger;
			DebugLogger val = (DebugLogger)(object)((logger is DebugLogger) ? logger : null);
			if (bDisplayMessageOnException && val != null && val.FilePath != null)
			{
				MMMessageBox.Show(string.Format(ApwnResources.PlotUnhandledException, val.FilePath), ApwnResources.AspenPlus, (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)16, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
			}
		}

		RibbonControlType IRibbonPlotWizardType.GetFieldCtrlType(string sField)
		{
			if (sField.CompareTo(_stageOrderFieldId) != 0 && sField.CompareTo(_stageOnFieldId) != 0)
			{
				return (RibbonControlType)0;
			}
			return (RibbonControlType)1;
		}

		void IRibbonPlotWizardType.GetFieldStyle(string sFieldName, ref string sCurrentVal)
		{
			if (sFieldName.CompareTo(_stageOrderFieldId) == 0)
			{
				if (_wizardPlot.PlotVal.ReverseXAxis)
				{
					sCurrentVal = _descending;
				}
				else
				{
					sCurrentVal = _ascending;
				}
			}
			else if (sFieldName.CompareTo(_stageOnFieldId) == 0)
			{
				if (_wizardPlot.PlotVal.SwapAxises)
				{
					sCurrentVal = _yAxis;
				}
				else
				{
					sCurrentVal = _xAxis;
				}
			}
		}

		void IRibbonPlotWizardType.SetFieldStyle(string sFieldName, string sValue)
		{
			if (sFieldName.CompareTo(_stageOrderFieldId) == 0)
			{
				if (sValue.CompareTo(_ascending) == 0)
				{
					_wizardPlot.PlotVal.ReverseXAxis = false;
				}
				else
				{
					_wizardPlot.PlotVal.ReverseXAxis = true;
				}
			}
			else if (sFieldName.CompareTo(_stageOnFieldId) == 0)
			{
				if (sValue.CompareTo(_yAxis) == 0)
				{
					_wizardPlot.PlotVal.SwapAxises = true;
				}
				else
				{
					_wizardPlot.PlotVal.SwapAxises = false;
				}
			}
		}

		void IRibbonPlotWizardType.GetConfigData(ref string sConfigValue)
		{
			sConfigValue = _currentConfigValue;
		}

		void IRibbonPlotWizardType.SetConfigData(string sValue)
		{
			_currentConfigValue = sValue;
		}

		void IRibbonPlotWizardType.Context(object HNode, ref object HWizardPlot, ref int ierror)
		{
			if (HWizardPlot == null || HNode == null)
			{
				ierror = 1;
				return;
			}
			_context = HNode as IHNode;
			_wizardPlot = HWizardPlot as IHWizardPlot;
			if (1 == 0)
			{
				ierror = 1;
				return;
			}
			string text = "";
			GetNStage();
			GetBlockID();
			switch (text)
			{
			case "Vapor-Liquid":
			case "Vapor-Liquid-LiquidCondenser":
			case "Vapor-Liquid-FreeWaterCondenser":
				_phaseRadBtnList[0].IsEnabled = true;
				_phaseRadBtnList[1].IsEnabled = true;
				break;
			case "Vapor-Liquid-Liquid":
				_phaseRadBtnList[0].IsEnabled = true;
				_phaseRadBtnList[1].IsEnabled = true;
				_phaseRadBtnList[2].IsEnabled = true;
				_phaseRadBtnList[3].IsEnabled = true;
				break;
			case "Vapor-Liquid-FreeWater":
				_phaseRadBtnList[0].IsEnabled = true;
				_phaseRadBtnList[1].IsEnabled = true;
				_phaseRadBtnList[2].IsEnabled = true;
				break;
			default:
				_phaseRadBtnList[0].IsEnabled = true;
				_phaseRadBtnList[1].IsEnabled = true;
				break;
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_currentConfigValue))
			{
				return;
			}
			_basisRadBtnList[0].IsChecked = true;
			for (int i = 0; i < _phaseRadBtnList.Count(); i++)
			{
				if (_phaseRadBtnList[i].IsEnabled)
				{
					_phaseRadBtnList[i].IsChecked = true;
					break;
				}
			}
		}

		public void UpdateWizardData()
		{
			int num = -1;
			string text = "";
			string text2 = "";
			for (int i = 0; i < _phaseRadBtnList.Count; i++)
			{
				if (_phaseRadBtnList[i].IsChecked == true)
				{
					num = i;
					text = _strTitle[i];
					text2 = _strYTitle[i];
					break;
				}
			}
			_sTitle = text;
			int num2 = -1;
			string text3 = string.Empty;
			string sTitleID = string.Empty;
			for (int j = 0; j < _basisRadBtnList.Count; j++)
			{
				if (_basisRadBtnList[j].IsChecked == true)
				{
					num2 = j;
					sTitleID = _strTBasis[j];
					text3 = _strYAxisTitle[j];
					break;
				}
			}
			_sTitleID = sTitleID;
			_wizardPlot.basis = num2 + 1;
			_wizardPlot.RemoveAllDependentVariable();
			string text4 = _varNames[num2, num];
			if (text4 != "")
			{
				List<string> list = new List<string>(CurveList.GetSelectedCurves());
				int count = list.Count;
				_VarName = text4;
				_selectedCurves.Clear();
				for (int k = 0; k < count; k++)
				{
					_selectedCurves.Add(list[k]);
				}
				int nStage = GetNStage();
				double[] array = new double[nStage];
				_wizardPlot.PlotVal.NXVariables = 1;
				_wizardPlot.PlotVal.NVariables = count;
				_wizardPlot.PlotVal.NPoints = nStage;
				for (int l = 0; l < count; l++)
				{
					string text5 = list[l];
					((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_Label(l, text5);
					string text6 = text4 + "(\"" + text5 + "\")";
					array[0] = _mvarControlContext.Condenser.resolve(text6).value;
					for (int m = 2; m < nStage; m++)
					{
						array[m - 1] = _mvarControlContext.Stage(m).Resolve(text6).value;
					}
					array[nStage - 1] = _mvarControlContext.Pot.resolve(text6).value;
					for (int n = 0; n < nStage; n++)
					{
						double num3 = n + 1;
						((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_XData(0, n, (short)0, (object)num3);
						((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_YData(l, n, (short)0, (object)array[n]);
					}
				}
			}
			_wizardPlot.PlotVal.Title = text;
			((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_YAxisTitle((object)null, text2 + " " + text3);
			_currentConfigValue = SaveConfigValue();
		}

		private List<string> GetListofFeedNames()
		{
			string text = null;
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			object nodeCxs = _nodeCxs;
			CxsNode val = (CxsNode)((nodeCxs is CxsNode) ? nodeCxs : null);
			if (val != null)
			{
				CxsCase val2 = val.Case;
				string path = val.Path;
				string text2 = path + ".Input.CHARGE_STRM";
				object obj = val2.BatchGet(text2, "value");
				if (obj != null)
				{
					text = ((obj != DBNull.Value) ? ((string)val2.BatchGet(text2, "value")) : string.Empty);
				}
				_pathTofeedStages = path + ".Input.FEED_STAGE";
				object obj2 = val2.BatchGet(_pathTofeedStages, "child_ids");
				if (obj2 != null)
				{
					list = (from x in obj2.ToString().Split()
						where !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(x)
						select x).ToList();
				}
				if (!text.Equals(string.Empty))
				{
					list.Add(text);
				}
			}
			return list;
		}

			for (int num2 = 0; num2 < SelectedVariablesDataGrid.Items.Count; num2++)

						text6 = text6.Substring(0, num5);
						s_datagridList.Add(text6);
					}
					else
					{

									if (text8.Equals(list[num10]))

										text = ((!text9.Equals(WpfPLBatchSepResources.Condenser) && !text9.Equals(WpfPLBatchSepResources.Pot)) ? ((!text9.Equals(WpfPLBatchSepResources.SecondCondenser)) ? ((!selectedVariables2.Contains(WpfPLBatchSepResources.RefluxDrum)) ? ((!selectedVariables2.Contains(WpfPLBatchSepResources.Stage)) ? ((!selectedVariables2.Contains(WpfPLBatchSepResources.DistillateReceiver)) ? ((!selectedVariables2.Contains(WpfPLBatchSepResources.SideDrawReceiver)) ? (text + "$" + value + "(*," + list[num10] + ")") : (text + "$SDRECEIVERS(" + text4 + ")_" + value + "(" + list[num10] + ")")) : (text + "$DISTRECV(" + text4 + ")_" + value + "(" + list[num10] + ")")) : (text + "$" + value + "(" + text4 + "," + list[num10] + ")")) : (text + "$" + value + "(0," + list[num10] + ")")) : (text + "$" + value + "(" + list[num10] + ")")) : (text + "$" + value + "(" + list[num10] + ")"));
										break;
									}
								}
							}
							else
							{
								text = ((!text9.Equals(WpfPLBatchSepResources.SecondCondenser)) ? ((!selectedVariables2.Contains(WpfPLBatchSepResources.RefluxDrum)) ? ((!selectedVariables2.Contains(WpfPLBatchSepResources.Stage)) ? ((!selectedVariables2.Contains(WpfPLBatchSepResources.DistillateReceiver)) ? ((!selectedVariables2.Contains(WpfPLBatchSepResources.SideDrawReceiver)) ? ((!selectedVariables2.Contains(WpfPLBatchSepResources.JacketHeating + "." + WpfPLBatchSepResources.Duty) && !selectedVariables2.Contains(WpfPLBatchSepResources.JacketCooling + "." + WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_Duty)) ? ((!selectedVariables2.Contains(WpfPLBatchSepResources.CoilsHeating + "." + WpfPLBatchSepResources.Duty) && !selectedVariables2.Contains(WpfPLBatchSepResources.CoilsCooling + "." + WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_Duty)) ? ((!selectedVariables2.Contains(WpfPLBatchSepResources.ExchangerHeating + "." + WpfPLBatchSepResources.Duty) && !selectedVariables2.Contains(WpfPLBatchSepResources.ExchangerCooling + "." + WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_Duty)) ? ((selectedVariables2.Contains(WpfPLBatchSepResources.JacketHeating) && !selectedVariables2.Contains(WpfPLBatchSepResources.JacketHeating + "." + WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_Duty)) ? ((!selectedVariables2.Contains(WpfPLBatchSepResources.JacketHeating + "." + WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_MediumOutletTemperature)) ? (text + "$" + value + "HEATJACKET_CALC") : (text + "$" + value + "HEATJACKET")) : ((selectedVariables2.Contains(WpfPLBatchSepResources.JacketCooling) && !selectedVariables2.Contains(WpfPLBatchSepResources.JacketCooling + "." + WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_Duty)) ? ((!selectedVariables2.Contains(WpfPLBatchSepResources.JacketCooling + "." + WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_MediumOutletTemperature)) ? (text + "$" + value + "COOLJACKET_CALC") : (text + "$" + value + "COOLJACKET")) : ((selectedVariables2.Contains(WpfPLBatchSepResources.CoilsHeating) && !selectedVariables2.Contains(WpfPLBatchSepResources.CoilsHeating + "." + WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_Duty)) ? ((!selectedVariables2.Contains(WpfPLBatchSepResources.CoilsHeating + "." + WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_MediumOutletTemperature)) ? 		private void SetVariables()
		{
			_varNames = new string[2, 4]
			{
				{ "Y", "X", "X1", "X2" },
				{ "YM", "XM", "XM1", "XM2" }
			};
			_strTitle = new string[4]
			{
				WpfPLBatchSepResources.Composition_Title_VaporA + " " + WpfPLBatchSepResources.Profile,
				WpfPLBatchSepResources.Composition_Title_LiquidA + " " + WpfPLBatchSepResources.Profile,
				WpfPLBatchSepResources.Composition_Title_1stLiquidA + " " + WpfPLBatchSepResources.Profile,
				WpfPLBatchSepResources.Composition_Title_2ndLiquidA + " " + WpfPLBatchSepResources.Profile
			};
			_strTBasis = new string[2]
			{
				WpfPLBatchSepResources.Mole,
				WpfPLBatchSepResources.Mass
			};
			_strYTitle = new string[4] { "Y", "X", "X1", "X2" };
			_strYAxisTitle = new string[2]
			{
				WpfPLBatchSepResources.Composition_Title_Mole,
				WpfPLBatchSepResources.Composition_Title_Mass
			};
		}

		private void SetControls()
		{
			_phaseRadBtnList = new List<RadioButton>();
			_phaseRadBtnList.Add(radBtnVapor);
			_phaseRadBtnList.Add(radBtnLiquid);
			_phaseRadBtnList.Add(radBtn1stLiquid);
			_phaseRadBtnList.Add(radBtn2ndLiquid);
			for (int i = 0; i < _phaseRadBtnList.Count; i++)
			{
				_phaseRadBtnList[i].IsEnabled = false;
				_phaseRadBtnList[i].IsChecked = false;
			}
			_basisRadBtnList = new List<RadioButton>();
			_basisRadBtnList.Add(radBtnMole);
			_basisRadBtnList.Add(radBtnMass);
		}

		private string SaveConfigValue()
		{
			_ = string.Empty;
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			stringBuilder.Append("PhaseOption=");
			for (int i = 0; i < _phaseRadBtnList.Count; i++)
			{
				if (_phaseRadBtnList[i].IsChecked == true)
				{
					stringBuilder.Append(i.ToString());
					break;
				}
			}
			stringBuilder.Append(";");
			stringBuilder.Append("PhaseEnable=");
			int num = 0;
			for (int j = 0; j < _phaseRadBtnList.Count; j++)
			{
				if (_phaseRadBtnList[j].IsEnabled)
				{
					if (num > 0)
					{
						stringBuilder.Append(",");
					}
					stringBuilder.Append(j.ToString());
					num++;
				}
			}
			stringBuilder.Append(";");
			stringBuilder.Append("BasisOption=");
			for (int k = 0; k < _basisRadBtnList.Count; k++)
			{
				if (_basisRadBtnList[k].IsChecked == true)
				{
					stringBuilder.Append(k.ToString());
					break;
				}
			}
			stringBuilder.Append(";");
			stringBuilder.Append("BasisEnable=");
			num = 0;
			for (int l = 0; l < _basisRadBtnList.Count; l++)
			{
				if (_basisRadBtnList[l].IsEnabled)
				{
					if (num > 0)
					{
						stringBuilder.Append(",");
					}
					stringBuilder.Append(l.ToString());
					num++;
				}
			}
			stringBuilder.Append(";");
			stringBuilder.Append("CompSelection=");
			List<string> list = new List<string>(CurveList.GetSelectedCurves());
			int count = list.Count;
			for (int m = 0; m < count; m++)
			{
				if (m > 0)
				{
					stringBuilder.Append(",");
				}
				string value = list[m];
				stringBuilder.Append(value);
			}
			stringBuilder.Append(";");
			stringBuilder.Append("CheckAll=");
			stringBuilder.Append(CurveList.IsCheckAll);
			stringBuilder.Append(";");
			return stringBuilder.ToString();
		}

		private void RestoreConfigValue(string ConfigString)
		{
			IHNode iHNode = null;
			string[] array = ConfigString.Split(';');
			int num = 0;
			string value = array[num].Substring(array[num].IndexOf('=') + 1);
			int num2 = Convert.ToInt16(value);
			_phaseRadBtnList[num2].IsChecked = true;
			iHNode = _context.FindNode(_varNames[0, num2]);
			num++;
			value = array[num].Substring(array[num].IndexOf('=') + 1);
			string[] array2 = value.Split(',');
			foreach (string value2 in array2)
			{
				_phaseRadBtnList[Convert.ToInt16(value2)].IsEnabled = true;
			}
			num++;
			value = array[num].Substring(array[num].IndexOf('=') + 1);
			_basisRadBtnList[Convert.ToInt16(value)].IsChecked = true;
			num++;
			value = array[num].Substring(array[num].IndexOf('=') + 1);
			array2 = value.Split(',');
			foreach (string value3 in array2)
			{
				_basisRadBtnList[Convert.ToInt16(value3)].IsEnabled = true;
			}
			num++;
			value = array[num].Substring(array[num].IndexOf('=') + 1);
			string[] array3 = value.Split(',');
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			if (iHNode != null && iHNode.Elements.Count > 0)
			{
				IHNodeCol elements = iHNode.Elements[0, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements;
				if (elements != null && elements.Count > 0)
				{
					for (int j = 0; j < elements.Count; j++)
					{
						string item = elements.get_Label(0, j, (object)null);
						list.Add(item);
					}
					CurveList.IsCheckAll = false;
					CurveList.SetCurves(list.ToArray());
				}
			}
			List<string> list2 = new List<string>();
			array2 = array3;
			foreach (string item2 in array2)
			{
				list2.Add(item2);
			}
			CurveList.SetSelectedCurves(list2.ToArray());
			num++;
			value = array[num].Substring(array[num].IndexOf('=') + 1);
			CurveList.IsCheckAll = Convert.ToBoolean(value);
		}

		private void ExecuteOKCommand(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			UpdateWizardData();
			_ = _currentConfigValue;
			_currentConfigValue = GetTimeConfig();
			_help.CloseForm((UserControl)this, (bool?)true);
		}

		private void CanExecuteOKCommand(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			int num = CurveList.GetSelectedCurves().Count();
			if (num == CurveList.Curves.Count)
			{
				CurveList.IsCheckAll = true;
			}
			else
			{
				string[] selectedCurves = CurveList.GetSelectedCurves();
				CurveList.IsCheckAll = false;
				CurveList.SetSelectedCurves(selectedCurves);
			}
			e.CanExecute = num != 0;
		}

		private void ExecuteCancelCommand(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			_currentConfigValue = string.Empty;
			_sYAxisName = string.Empty;
			_sTitle = string.Empty;
			_help.CloseForm((UserControl)this, (bool?)false);
		}

		private void AddButton_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			foreach (DataRow selectedRow in gcVariables.SelectedRows)
			{
				foreach (Selected_variablelist selectedVariable in BuildSelectedVariables(selectedRow, CategoriesTreeView.SelectedItem as TreeViewItem))
				{
					if (!obCollection_SelVars.Any((Selected_variablelist obj) => obj.SelectedVariables == selectedVariable.SelectedVariables))
					{
						obCollection_SelVars.Add(selectedVariable);
					}
				}
				if (selectedRow.GroupingRowsCount <= 0)
				{
					selectedRow.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				}
			}
			if (SelectedVariablesDataGrid.ItemsSource == null)
			{
				SelectedVariablesDataGrid.ItemsSource = obCollection_SelVars;
			}
			SelectedVariablesDataGrid.Items.SortDescriptions.Add(new SortDescription("Name", ListSortDirection.Ascending));
			DoneButton.IsEnabled = true;
		}

		private void RemoveButton_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (SelectedVariablesDataGrid == null || SelectedVariablesDataGrid.SelectedItem == null || gcVariables == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			IList selectedItems = SelectedVariablesDataGrid.SelectedItems;
			foreach (Selected_variablelist item in selectedItems)
			{
				foreach (DataRow item2 in gcVariables.DataSource.Rows.Where((DataRow row) => row.Tag == item.DataGridrowTag))
				{
					item2.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
				}
			}
			while (selectedItems.Count > 0)
			{
				obCollection_SelVars.Remove((Selected_variablelist)selectedItems[0]);
			}
			SelectedVariablesDataGrid.ItemsSource = obCollection_SelVars;
			if (SelectedVariablesDataGrid.Items.Count == 0)
			{
				DoneButton.IsEnabled = false;
			}
		}

		private void DoneButton_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			_ifDonebutton = true;
			UpdateWizardData();
			_currentConfigValue = GetTimeConfig();
			_help.CloseForm((UserControl)this, (bool?)true);
		}

		private void gcVariables_SizeChanged(object sender, SizeChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			DataGridColumn dataGridColumn = ((DataGrid)(object)gcVariables.DataGridControlInstance)?.Columns?.FirstOrDefault();
			if (dataGridColumn != null)
			{
				dataGridColumn.Width = ((FrameworkElement)(object)gcVariables).ActualWidth - 45.0;
			}
		}

		private void gcVariables_SelectionChanged(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (gcVariables == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (gcVariables.SelectedCells == null || gcVariables.SelectedRows.Count() == 0)
			{
				AddButton.IsEnabled = false;
				return;
			}
			AddButton.IsEnabled = gcVariables.SelectedRows.All((DataRow row) => Isrow_notParent(row));
		}

		private void gcVariables_CellValueQueried(object sender, CellValueQueriedEventArgs e)
		{
			string text = (e.Row.Tag as Tuple<string>).Item1;
			if (text.Count((char cdot) => cdot == '.') == 1)
			{
				text = text.Substring(text.LastIndexOf('.') + 1);
			}
			if (e.Column.Header == WpfPLBatchSepResources.Variables)
			{
				if (text.Equals(_strHCDuty) || text.Equals(_strHDuty) || text.Equals(_strCDuty))
				{
					e.Data = DataRow.CreateDataCell((object)_strDuty, false, (IEnumerable)null, (CellUIType)0, (object)null, (System.Windows.Media.Brush)null, (Type)null, (string)null, (string)null, (HorizontalAlignment?)null, (string)null, (Thickness?)null, (System.Windows.Media.Brush)null, false);
				}
				else
				{
					e.Data = DataRow.CreateDataCell((object)text, false, (IEnumerable)null, (CellUIType)0, (object)null, (System.Windows.Media.Brush)null, (Type)null, (string)null, (string)null, (HorizontalAlignment?)null, (string)null, (Thickness?)null, (System.Windows.Media.Brush)null, false);
				}
			}
		}

		private void UpdateVariableDescriptionColumnWidth(double delta, double? newWidth = null)
		{
			if (gcVariables == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			DataColumn variableDescriptionColumn = GetVariableDescriptionColumn();
			if (variableDescriptionColumn != null && variableDescriptionColumn.Width.HasValue)
			{
				if (newWidth.HasValue)
				{
					variableDescriptionColumn.Width = newWidth;
				}
				else
				{
					variableDescriptionColumn.Width += delta;
				}
			}
			gcVariables.RefreshData();
		}

		private DataColumn GetVariableDescriptionColumn()
		{
			if (gcVariables == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			DataTable dataSource = gcVariables.DataSource;
			if (dataSource == null || dataSource.Columns == null || dataSource.Columns.Count() == 0)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return dataSource.Columns.Last();
		}

			List<string> childnode = new List<string>();

		}

		private DataTable CreateGridControl_Variables(List<Variablelist> entire_vlist, int count)
		{
			//IL_001c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0021: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0032: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0041: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_004d: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0051: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0057: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_00a1: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00a6: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00c2: Expected O, but got Unknown
			TreeViewItem treeViewItem = CategoriesTreeView.SelectedItem as TreeViewItem;
			List<DataColumn> list = new List<DataColumn>
			{
				new DataColumn(WpfPLBatchSepResources.Variables)
				{
					Width = 400.0,
					MinWidth = 200.0,
					IsReadOnly = true
				}
			};
			int num = 0;
			DataTable val = new DataTable((IEnumerable<DataColumn>)list);
			foreach (Variablelist item in entire_vlist)
			{
				if (item.childnode.Count > 0)
				{
					num = entire_vlist.IndexOf(item);
					int sub_childcount = 0;
					AddRows_TochildVariables(val, ref sub_childcount, num);
				}
				else
				{
					num = entire_vlist.IndexOf(item);
					val.Rows.Add(new DataRow((IEnumerable<DataColumn>)list)
					{
						Tag = new Tuple<string>(entire_vlist[num].VariablesName)
					});
				}
			}
			getValidPhases_selection(val);
			if (treeViewItem == JacketHeatingTVI)
			{
				getJacketHeatingOption(val);
			}
			if (treeViewItem == JacketCoolingTVI)
			{
				getJacketCoolingOption(val);
			}
			if (treeViewItem == CoilsHeatingTVI)
			{
				getCoilsHeatingOption(val);
			}
			if (treeViewItem == CoilsCoolingTVI)
			{
				getCoilsCoolingOption(val);
			}
			if (treeViewItem == ExchangerHeatingTVI)
			{
				getEXHXHeatingOption(val);
			}
			if (treeViewItem == ExchangerCoolingTVI)
			{
				getEXHXCoolingOption(val);
			}
			return val;
		}

		private void AddRows_TochildVariables(DataTable tableofVariables, ref int sub_childcount, int parentindex)
		{
			//IL_000c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0011: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_002e: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0055: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_005a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_009d: Expected O, but got Unknown
			sub_childcount++;
			DataRow val = new DataRow((IEnumerable<DataColumn>)tableofVariables.Columns)
			{
				Tag = new Tuple<string>(entire_variableList[parentindex].VariablesName)
			};
			tableofVariables.Rows.Add(val);
			if (entire_variableList[parentindex] != null)
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < entire_variableList[parentindex].childnode.Count; i++)
				{
					DataRow item = new DataRow((IEnumerable<DataColumn>)tableofVariables.Columns)
					{
						Tag = new Tuple<string>(entire_variableList[parentindex].VariablesName + "." + entire_variableList[parentindex].childnode[i].ToString())
					};
					tableofVariables.Rows.Add(item);
				}
				val.GroupingRowsCount = entire_variableList[parentindex].childnode.Count;
				if (entire_variableList[parentindex].childnode.Count > 0)
				{
					val.GroupingState = (RowGroupingState)1;
				}
				else
				{
					val.GroupingState = (RowGroupingState)0;
				}
				sub_childcount = entire_variableList[parentindex].childnode.Count;
			}
		}

		private bool Isrow_notParent(DataRow row)
		{
			if (row.GroupingRowsCount > 0 || row.Tag == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

				Variablelist variablelist53 = new Variablelist(WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_MoleFlow, null);
				variablelist53.label_selectedRow.Add(treeViewItem.Header?.ToString() + "." + variablelist53.VariablesName);
				list.Add(variablelist53);
				Variablelist variablelist54 = new Variablelist(WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_VolumeFlow, null);
				variablelist54.label_selectedRow.Add(treeViewItem.Header?.ToString() + "." + variablelist54.VariablesName);
				list.Add(variablelist54);
			}
			gcVariables.DataSource = CreateGridControl_Variables(list, count);
			gcVariables.FreezeColumn(gcVariables.DataSource.Columns.First());
			gcVariables.CellValueQueried += gcVariables_CellValueQueried;
		}

		private List<Selected_variablelist> BuildSelectedVariables(DataRow rowItem, TreeViewItem selectedTreeNode)
		{
			List<Selected_variablelist> list = new List<Selected_variablelist>();
			string stringBetweenParentheses = rowItem.Tag.ToString().GetStringBetweenParentheses();
			string selectedTreeNodeHeader = selectedTreeNode.Header.ToString();
			Selected_variablelist selected_variablelist = null;
			string empty = string.Empty;
			switch (selectedTreeNode.Name)
			{
			case "StageTVI":
				if (IsAllStagesChecked)
				{
					foreach (object item in (IEnumerable)CB_Stages.Items)
					{
						empty = item.ToString();
						selected_variablelist = new Selected_variablelist(selectedTreeNodeHeader, empty, stringBetweenParentheses, rowItem);
						list.Add(selected_variablelist);
					}
				}
				else
				{
					empty = CB_Stages.SelectionBoxItem.ToString();
					selected_variablelist = new Selected_variablelist(selectedTreeNodeHeader, empty, stringBetweenParentheses, rowItem);
					list.Add(selected_variablelist);
				}
				break;
			case "DistillateReceiversTVI":
				empty = CB_Receiver.SelectionBoxItem.ToString();
				selected_variablelist = new Selected_variablelist(selectedTreeNodeHeader, empty, stringBetweenParentheses, rowItem);
				list.Add(selected_variablelist);
				break;
			case "SideDrawReceiverTVI":
				empty = CB_SideDrawReceiver.SelectionBoxItem.ToString();
				selected_variablelist = new Selected_variablelist(selectedTreeNodeHeader, empty, stringBetweenParentheses, rowItem);
				list.Add(selected_variablelist);
				break;
			case "FeedsTVI":
				empty = CB_FeedNames.SelectionBoxItem.ToString();
				selected_variablelist = new Selected_variablelist(selectedTreeNodeHeader, empty, stringBetweenParentheses, rowItem);
				list.Add(selected_variablelist);
				break;
			default:
			{
				empty = string.Empty;
				string lastSegment = ((stringBetweenParentheses.Equals(_strHCDuty) || stringBetweenParentheses.Equals(_strHDuty) || stringBetweenParentheses.Equals(_strCDuty)) ? _strDuty : stringBetweenParentheses);
				selected_variablelist = new Selected_variablelist(selectedTreeNodeHeader, empty, lastSegment, rowItem);
				list.Add(selected_variablelist);
				break;
			}
			}
			return list;
		}

		public void InitializeComponent()
		{
			if (!_contentLoaded)
			{
				_contentLoaded = true;
				Uri resourceLocator = new Uri("/WpfPLBatchSep;component/customplot/customstripplot.xaml", UriKind.Relative);
				Application.LoadComponent(this, resourceLocator);
			}
		}

		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "4.0.0.0")]
		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		void IStyleConnector.Connect(int connectionId, object target)
		{
			switch (connectionId)
			{
			case 46:
			{
				EventSetter eventSetter = new EventSetter();
				eventSetter.Event = UIElement.MouseDownEvent;
				eventSetter.Handler = new MouseButtonEventHandler(AllVariablesDataGridCell_MouseDown);
				((Style)target).Setters.Add(eventSetter);
				break;
			}
			case 50:
			{
				EventSetter eventSetter = new EventSetter();
				eventSetter.Event = UIElement.MouseDownEvent;
				eventSetter.Handler = new MouseButtonEventHandler(SelectedVariablesDataGridCell_MouseDown);
				((Style)target).Setters.Add(eventSetter);
				break;
			}
			}
		}

	internal class Selected_variablelist : Variablelist
	{
		public string SelectedVariables { get; set; }

		public object DataGridrowTag { get; set; }

		public Selected_variablelist(string selectedTreeNodeHeader, string treeNodeSegment, string lastSegment, DataRow rowItem)
		{
			SelectedVariables = BuildVariableId(selectedTreeNodeHeader, treeNodeSegment, lastSegment);
			if (rowItem.GroupingRowsCount <= 0)
			{
				DataGridrowTag = rowItem.Tag;
			}
		}

		private static string BuildVariableId(string selectedTreeNodeHeader, string treeNodeSegment, string lastSegment)
		{
			treeNodeSegment = (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(treeNodeSegment) ? string.Empty : ("(" + treeNodeSegment + ")"));
			return selectedTreeNodeHeader + treeNodeSegment + "." + lastSegment;
		}
	}
	internal class RowVisibilitySetter
	{
		internal void SetForValidPhasesOption(string validPhase, DataTable allVariables)
		{
			switch (validPhase)
			{
			case "Liquid-Only":
			{
				string[] list = new string[11]
				{
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_VaporMass,
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_VaporMoles,
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_VaporVolume,
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_VaporDensity,
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_VaporViscosity,
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_Liquid1Mass,
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_Liquid2Mass,
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_Liquid1Moles,
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_Liquid2Moles,
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_Liquid1Volume,
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_Liquid2Volume
				};
				string[] list4 = new string[6]
				{
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_VMoleF,
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_VMassF,
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_L1MassF,
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_L2MassF,
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_L1MoleF,
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_L2MoleF
				};
				for (int num = 0; num < allVariables.Rows.Count; num++)
				{
					string tag4 = allVariables.Rows[num].Tag.ToString();
					if (list.ContainsTag(tag4))
					{
						allVariables.Rows[num].Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
					}
					else if (list4.ContainsTag(tag4))
					{
						allVariables.Rows[num].Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
						for (int num2 = 1; num2 <= allVariables.Rows[num].GroupingRowsCount; num2++)
						{
							allVariables.Rows[num + num2].Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
						}
					}
				}
				break;
			}
			case "Vapor-Liquid":
			{
				string[] list = new string[20]
				{

					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_L2MassF,
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_L1MoleF,
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_L2MoleF
				};
				for (int k = 0; k < allVariables.Rows.Count; k++)
				{
					string tag2 = allVariables.Rows[k].Tag.ToString();
					if (list.ContainsTag(tag2))
					{
						allVariables.Rows[k].Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
					}
					else if (list4.ContainsTag(tag2))
					{
						allVariables.Rows[k].Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
						for (int l = 1; l <= allVariables.Rows[k].GroupingRowsCount; l++)
						{
							allVariables.Rows[k + l].Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
						}

			case "Vapor-Liquid-LiquidCondenser":
			case "Vapor-Liquid-FreeWaterCondenser":

					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_L1MassRefluxRate,
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_L2MassRefluxRate
				};

					}
					else if (list2.ContainsTag(tag3))
					{
						allVariables.Rows[m].Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
					}
					else if (list5.ContainsTag(tag3))
					{
						allVariables.Rows[m].Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
						for (int n = 1; n <= allVariables.Rows[m].GroupingRowsCount; n++)
						{
							allVariables.Rows[m + n].Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
