			}
			default:
				SetVisibility(ref allVariables, Visibility.Visible);
				break;
			}
		}

		internal void SetForExchangerHeatingOptions(string selectedOption, DataTable allVariables, CxsNode cxsNode)
		{
			switch (selectedOption)
			{
			case "SPECIFIED MEDIUM TEMPERATURE":
			{
				string[] array = new string[4]
				{
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_MediumMassFlowRate,
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_MediumInletTemperature,
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_MediumOutletTemperature,
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_SteamMassFlow
				};
				for (int l = 0; l < allVariables.Rows.Count; l++)
				{
					string tag3 = allVariables.Rows[l].Tag.ToString();
					if (array.ContainsTag(tag3))
					{
						allVariables.Rows[l].Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
					}
				}
				break;
			}
			case "LMTD":
			{
				string[] array = new string[2]
				{
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_MediumTemperature,
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_SteamMassFlow
				};
				for (int n = 0; n < allVariables.Rows.Count; n++)
				{
					string tag5 = allVariables.Rows[n].Tag.ToString();
					if (array.ContainsTag(tag5))
					{
						allVariables.Rows[n].Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
					}
				}
				break;
			}
			case "STEAM HEATING":
			{
				string text = null;
				string text2 = cxsNode.Path + ".Input.CH_STEAMLIMT";
				if (cxsNode.Case.BatchGet(text2, "value") != null)
				{
					text = (string)cxsNode.Case.BatchGet(text2, "value");
				}
				string[] array = null;
				if (text == "HEAT")
				{
					array = new string[4]
					{
						WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_MediumTemperature,
						WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_MediumMassFlowRate,
						WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_MediumOutletTemperature,
						WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_MediumInletTemperature
					};
				}
				else if (text == "DUTY")
				{
					array = new string[5]
					{
						WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_MediumTemperature,
						WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_MediumMassFlowRate,
						WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_MediumOutletTemperature,
						WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_MediumInletTemperature,
						WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_SteamMassFlow
					};
				}
				for (int j = 0; j < allVariables.Rows.Count; j++)
				{
					string tag = allVariables.Rows[j].Tag.ToString();
					if (array != null && array.ContainsTag(tag))
					{
						allVariables.Rows[j].Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
					}
				}
				break;
			}
			case "SPECIFIED DUTY":
			{
				string[] array = new string[5]
				{
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_MediumTemperature,
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_MediumMassFlowRate,
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_MediumInletTemperature,
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_MediumOutletTemperature,
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_SteamMassFlow
				};
				for (int m = 0; m < allVariables.Rows.Count; m++)
				{
					string tag4 = allVariables.Rows[m].Tag.ToString();
					if (array.ContainsTag(tag4))
					{
						allVariables.Rows[m].Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
					}
				}
				break;
			}
			case "MASS BOILUP RATE":
			case "MOLE BOILUP RATE":
			case "FRACTION VAPORIZED":
			{
				string[] array = new string[6]
				{
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_MediumTemperature,
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_MediumMassFlowRate,
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_MediumInletTemperature,
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_MediumOutletTemperature,
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_SteamMassFlow,
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_RecirculationRate
				};
				for (int k = 0; k < allVariables.Rows.Count; k++)
				{
					string tag2 = allVariables.Rows[k].Tag.ToString();
					if (array.ContainsTag(tag2))
					{
						allVariables.Rows[k].Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
					}
				}
				break;
			}
			default:
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < allVariables.Rows.Count - 1; i++)
				{
					allVariables.Rows[i].Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
				}
				break;
			}
			}
		}

		internal void SetForJacketHeatingOptions(string selectedOption, DataTable allVariables, CxsNode cxsNode)
		{
			int num = allVariables.Rows.Count - 1;
			switch (selectedOption)
			{
			case "SPECIFIED MEDIUM TEMPERATURE":
			{
				string[] array = new string[4]
				{
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_MediumMassFlowRate,
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_MediumInletTemperature,
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_MediumOutletTemperature,
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_SteamMassFlow
				};
				for (int l = 0; l <= num; l++)
				{
					string tag3 = allVariables.Rows[l].Tag.ToString();
					if (array.ContainsTag(tag3))
					{
						allVariables.Rows[l].Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
					}
				}
				break;
			}
			case "LMTD":
			{
				string[] array = new string[2]
				{
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_MediumTemperature,
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_SteamMassFlow
				};
				for (int n = 0; n <= num; n++)
				{
					string tag5 = allVariables.Rows[n].Tag.ToString();
					if (array.ContainsTag(tag5))
					{
						allVariables.Rows[n].Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
					}
				}
				break;
			}
			case "STEAM HEATING":
			{
				string text = null;
				string text2 = cxsNode.Path + ".Input.JH_STEAMLIMT";
				if (cxsNode.Case.BatchGet(text2, "value") != null)
				{
					text = (string)cxsNode.Case.BatchGet(text2, "value");
				}
				string[] array = null;
				if (text == "HEAT")
				{
					array = new string[4]
					{
						WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_MediumTemperature,
						WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_MediumMassFlowRate,
						WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_MediumInletTemperature,
						WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_MediumOutletTemperature
					};
				}
				else if (text == "DUTY")
				{
					array = new string[5]
					{
						WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_MediumTemperature,
						WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_MediumMassFlowRate,
						WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_MediumInletTemperature,
						WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_MediumOutletTemperature,
						WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_SteamMassFlow
					};
				}
				for (int j = 0; j <= num; j++)
				{
					string tag = allVariables.Rows[j].Tag.ToString();
					if (array != null && array.ContainsTag(tag))
					{
						allVariables.Rows[j].Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
					}
				}
				break;
			}
			case "SPECIFIED DUTY":
			{
				string[] array = new string[5]
				{
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_MediumTemperature,
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_MediumMassFlowRate,
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_MediumInletTemperature,
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_MediumOutletTemperature,
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_SteamMassFlow
				};
				for (int m = 0; m <= num; m++)
				{
					string tag4 = allVariables.Rows[m].Tag.ToString();
					if (array.ContainsTag(tag4))
					{
						allVariables.Rows[m].Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
					}
				}
				break;
			}
			case "MASS BOILUP RATE":
			case "MOLE BOILUP RATE":
			case "FRACTION VAPORIZED":
			{
				string[] array = new string[6]
				{
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_MediumTemperature,
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_MediumMassFlowRate,
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_MediumInletTemperature,
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_MediumOutletTemperature,
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_SteamMassFlow,
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_RecirculationRate
				};
				for (int k = 0; k <= num; k++)
				{
					string tag2 = allVariables.Rows[k].Tag.ToString();
					if (array.ContainsTag(tag2))
					{
						allVariables.Rows[k].Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
					}
				}
				break;
			}
			default:
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < num; i++)
				{
					allVariables.Rows[i].Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
				}
				break;
			}
			}
		}

		internal void SetForJachekCoolingOptions(string selectedOption, DataTable allVariables)
		{
			int num = allVariables.Rows.Count - 1;
			switch (selectedOption)
			{
			case "SPECIFIED MEDIUM TEMPERATURE":
			{
				string[] list = new string[3]
				{
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_MediumMassFlowRate,
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_MediumInletTemperature,
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_MediumOutletTemperature
				};
				for (int j = 0; j <= num; j++)
				{
					string tag = allVariables.Rows[j].Tag.ToString();
					if (list.ContainsTag(tag))
					{
						allVariables.Rows[j].Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
					}
				}
				break;
			}
			case "LMTD":
			{
				string[] list = new string[1] { WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_MediumTemperature };
				for (int l = 0; l <= num; l++)
				{
					string tag3 = allVariables.Rows[l].Tag.ToString();
					if (list.ContainsTag(tag3))
					{
						allVariables.Rows[l].Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
					}
				}
				break;
			}
			case "SPECIFIED DUTY":
			{
				string[] list = new string[4]
				{
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_MediumTemperature,
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_MediumMassFlowRate,
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_MediumInletTemperature,
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_MediumOutletTemperature
				};
				for (int m = 0; m <= num; m++)
				{
					string tag4 = allVariables.Rows[m].Tag.ToString();
					if (list.ContainsTag(tag4))
					{
						allVariables.Rows[m].Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
					}
				}
				break;
			}
			case "MASS BOILUP RATE":
			case "MOLE BOILUP RATE":
			case "FRACTION VAPORIZED":
			{
				string[] list = new string[6]
				{
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_MediumTemperature,
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_MediumMassFlowRate,
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_MediumInletTemperature,
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_MediumOutletTemperature,
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_SteamMassFlow,
					WpfPLBatchSepResources.CustomPlot_Vars_RecirculationRate
				};
				for (int k = 0; k <= num; k++)
				{
					string tag2 = allVariables.Rows[k].Tag.ToString();
					if (list.ContainsTag(tag2))
					{
						allVariables.Rows[k].Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
					}
				}
				break;
			}
			default:
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < num; i++)
				{
					allVariables.Rows[i].Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
				}
				break;
			}
			}
		}

		public void SetVisibility(ref DataTable allVariables, Visibility visibility, ICollection<string> tagsFilters = null)
		{
			DataTable obj = allVariables;
			int? num = ((obj == null) ? ((int?)null) : obj.Rows?.Count);
			if (!num.HasValue)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (tagsFilters == null || tagsFilters.Count < 1)
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < num.Value; i++)
				{
					allVariables.Rows[i].Visibility = visibility;
				}
				return;
			}
			for (int j = 0; j < num.Value; j++)
			{
				string tag = allVariables.Rows[j].Tag.ToString();
				if (tagsFilters.ContainsTag(tag))
				{
					allVariables.Rows[j].Visibility = visibility;
				}
			}
		}
	}
